{"parentUuid":null,"isSidechain":true,"promptId":"0268ad75-d9be-4c45-a80f-a1298654d281","agentId":"a1dbf50fd7cad1ed9","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":"Read-only exploration of the SIGIL repo at /home/kali/sigil. I am scoping a \"phone companion app\" that lets the owner's phone drive SIGIL running on their PC (approve actions, run commands, control gesture sessions, view state). I need to know what remote-triggerable surfaces already exist and how they authenticate, so the phone app reuses them rather than inventing new paths.\n\nInvestigate and report concrete file paths, class/function signatures, and the auth model for each:\n\n1. `sigil/ui/` — the Phase 7 WS-C \"glass cockpit\" loopback web app. Files: `server.py`, `actions.py`, `static/*`. Report: the FULL endpoint list (GET read-plane + POST action-plane), the two-plane security model (127.0.0.1 bind, Origin/Referer check, X-SIGIL-Token, Host allowlist, CSRF-by-proof), how `/api/action` does owner-signed approve/deny (does the private key stay server-side?), the SSE `/api/stream` live feed, `/api/classify` tier prediction, `/api/ask` KERNEL relay. This is the most likely thing the phone app extends/reuses. Quote the endpoint handlers and the auth checks.\n\n2. `sigil/voice/dispatch.py` — `KernelDispatch`/`send()` — how a natural-language ask is routed to the Rust KERNEL, tier-classified, and executed. Signature + what it returns.\n\n3. `sigil/agents/actor.py` (DELEGATE) `preview()`/`execute()` and `sigil/agents/operator.py` `preview()`/`execute()` — the plan→approve→execute transaction shape a phone would drive (queue on PC, approve on phone). How does a queued step surface for approval (the `pending()` list, the seq the phone signs)?\n\n4. `sigil/gesture/session.py` — the owner-armed gesture SessionGate: `arm()`/`disarm()`, the signed `gesture.session_armed`/`disarmed` records, how injection is authorized. Could a phone arm/disarm a gesture session remotely (is there an approval/signature path)?\n\n5. `sigil/perception/` — `recall.py`, `vision.py`, `camera_stream.py` — could a phone camera/mic feed the perception pipeline, or query \"what did I last see\"? What's the ingestion contract (Frame)?\n\n6. `sigil/mcp/server.py` — the 8 read-only MCP tools (memory.search, graph.query, episodic.range, commitments.due, contradictions.pending, ingest.status, etc.) — a phone app could surface these read-only. List the tools + their gating.\n\nFor each surface, state clearly: what it does, how it authenticates, whether it's loopback-only or reachable over WireGuard, and what a phone client would need to call it. Do not propose changes — map the ground truth and note any surface that has NO remote path today."},"uuid":"9ce5c970-3996-43e2-87af-7f9c245ee648","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:56:01.015Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"3cc275af-2c24-4d32-8cc7-6debf860b031","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a1dbf50fd7cad1ed9","attachment":{"type":"skill_listing","content":"- crucible: How to operate CRUCIBLE — the authorized, prove-don't-guess offensive-security engine in framework/v2. Read this BEFORE running an engagement/scan, re-verifying a finding, inspecting the event spine, reasoning about the veracity/anti-hallucination layer, the learning/metacognition core, or the safety stack. Use it when a task names CRUCIBLE, OBSIDIAN, `python3 -m framework.v2 <subcommand>`, findings/oracles/evidence certificates, the blackboard event spine, critics, reflection, or the reward/calibration loop.\n- deep-research: Deep research harness — fan-out web searches, fetch sources, adversarially verify claims, synthesize a cited report. - When the user wants a deep, multi-source, fact-checked research report on any topic. BEFORE invoking, check if the question is specific enough to research directly — if underspecified (e.g., \"what car to buy\" without budget/use-case/region), ask 2-3 clarifying questions to narrow scope. Then pass the refined question as args, weaving the answers in.\n- adversarial-sweep: Independent multi-lens adversarial re-attack of a defensive-oracle domain — the FORGE stage-9 companion to RED-PEN. Attack from disjoint lenses, refute each objection with a real repro, report only what survives. - Before merging any AEGIS/FORGE domain stream, alongside (not instead of) the red-pen agent. Pass the domain spec via args. On the email-auth stream it caught 3 CRITICAL/LOW defects PAST a RED-PEN PASS — near-zero-FP cannot be self-certified by one reviewer.\n- dataviz: Use this skill whenever you are about to create ANY chart, graph, plot, dashboard, or data visualization, in ANY output medium — an HTML or React artifact, inline SVG, plotting code in any library (matplotlib, plotly, d3, Recharts, …), an image/PNG you will render and upload, or a chart shared into Slack. Read it BEFORE writing the first line of chart code, choosing chart colors, building a stat tile / meter / KPI row, or laying out a dashboard. Produces visualizations that read as one system — elegant, accessible, consistent in light and dark — using a brand-neutral placeholder palette you swap for your own. Teaches a design-system-agnostic method: a form heuristic, a color formula with a runnable validator, mark specs, and interaction rules. A validated default palette is documented in `references/palette.md` — swap that file's values for your brand's. Triggers on: \"chart\", \"graph\", \"plot\", \"data viz\", \"visualization\", \"dashboard\", \"analytics\", \"visualize data\", \"categorical colors\", \"sequential / diverging palette\", \"stat tile\", \"sparkline\", \"heatmap\", \"legend\", \"axis\", \"tooltip\", \"chart colors\", \"color by series\".\n- artifact-design: Design guidance and fundamentals for Artifacts.\n- artifact-capabilities: Runtime capabilities a published Artifact page can be granted — behavior static HTML cannot provide on its own, such as the page reading live or connected data, keeping state shared across viewers, or updating and republishing itself. Serves this user's live capability roster and the typed call definitions. Load it whenever the user asks for an artifact needing any such runtime behavior.\n- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- verify: Verify that a code change actually does what it's supposed to by exercising it end-to-end and observing behavior — drive the affected flow, not just tests or typecheck. Run before committing nontrivial changes; bootstraps this repo's project verify skill if none exists yet. Don't invoke it on a diff that only touches tests, docs, or other code with no runtime surface to drive (a change to product source always has one) — there's nothing to observe.\n- code-review: Review the current diff for correctness bugs and reuse/simplification/efficiency cleanups at the given effort level (low/medium: fewer, high-confidence findings; high→max: broader coverage, may include uncertain findings; ultra: deep multi-agent review in the cloud (requires claude.ai account access)). Pass --comment to post findings as inline PR comments, or --fix to apply the findings to the working tree after the review.\n- simplify: Review the changed code for reuse, simplification, efficiency, and altitude cleanups, then apply the fixes. Quality only — it does not hunt for bugs; use /code-review for that.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled cloud agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring cloud agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Reference for the Claude API / Anthropic SDK — model ids, pricing, params, streaming, tool use, MCP, agents, caching, token counting, model migration.\nTRIGGER — read BEFORE opening the target file; don't skip because it \"looks like a one-liner\" — whenever: the prompt names Claude/Anthropic in any form (Claude, Anthropic, Fable, Opus, Sonnet, Haiku, `anthropic`, `@anthropic-ai`, `claude-*`, `us.anthropic.*`, `[1m]`); the user asks about an LLM (pricing/model choice/limits/caching) — never answer from memory; OR the task is LLM-shaped with provider unstated (agent/MCP/tool-definition/multi-agent/RAG/LLM-judge/computer-use; generate/summarize/extract/classify/rewrite/converse over NL; debugging refusals/cutoffs/streaming/tool-calls/tokens).\nSKIP only when another provider is being worked on (overrides all triggers): OpenAI/GPT/Gemini/Llama/Mistral/Cohere/Ollama named in the query; OR `grep -rE 'openai|langchain_openai|google.generativeai|genai|mistralai|cohere|ollama'` over the project hits (run this grep FIRST if no provider named — don't Read the file).\n- run: Launch and drive this project's app to see a change working. Mirrors the MCP server's posture: read-only over the spine +\n3\tprovenance on every atom, plus a CSRF-proof owner-signed action plane.\n4\t\n5\tSecurity model (the red-pen keystone):\n6\t  • binds 127.0.0.1 ONLY (never 0.0.0.0/public).\n7\t  • a session TOKEN is minted at startup and PRINTED TO THE TERMINAL (so only someone at the machine\n8\t    can drive it, and no web page can read it). The served page embeds it; a cross-origin page cannot.\n9\t  • READ plane (GET /api/*): requires the token (header `X-SIGIL-Token`, or `?token=` for SSE which\n10\t    can't set headers). Read/query only — EXCEPT `/api/ask`, which DISPATCHES a WARDEN-gated KERNEL\n11\t    query (a subprocess), so it carries the FULL action gate (token + Origin + Host), not just token.\n12\t  • ACTION plane (POST /api/action): requires the token AND an EXACT-MATCH loopback `Origin`/`Referer`\n13\t    AND a `Host` in the allowlist (defeats DNS-rebinding). Routes ONLY the closed owner-signed action\n14\t    set. The private key never touches the browser — the server signs (see `ui.actions`).\n15\t  • Static assets + the index bootstrap are token-free (they carry no secret; the token is injected\n16\t    into the page as a data attribute, unreadable cross-origin). A strict CSP + external `self` JS/CSS\n17\t    keeps the page functional AND locked down.\"\"\"\n18\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n19\t\n20\timport hmac\n21\timport json\n22\timport time\n23\tfrom http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, ThreadingHTTPServer\n24\tfrom pathlib import Path\n25\tfrom urllib.parse import parse_qs, urlparse\n26\t\n27\tfrom ..config import SPINE_PATH\n28\tfrom ..spine.store import SpineStore\n29\tfrom ..spine.tail import SpineTailer\n30\tfrom ..spine.verify import verify_record\n31\tfrom . import actions as _actions\n32\t\n33\t_STATIC = Path(__file__).parent / \"static\"\n34\t_CSP = \"default-src 'self'; base-uri 'none'; form-action 'none'; frame-ancestors 'none'\"\n35\t\n36\t\n37\tclass UIServer(ThreadingHTTPServer):\n38\t    daemon_threads = True\n39\t\n40\t    def __init__(self, addr, handler, *, token: str, spine_path: Path):\n41\t        super().__init__(addr, handler)\n42\t        self.token = token\n43\t        self.spine_path = spine_path\n44\t        port = self.server_address[1]              # the ACTUAL bound port (correct even for port 0)\n45\t        self.allowed_hosts = frozenset({f\"127.0.0.1:{port}\", f\"localhost:{port}\"})\n46\t        self.allowed_origins = frozenset({f\"http://127.0.0.1:{port}\", f\"http://localhost:{port}\"})\n47\t\n48\t    def store(self) -> SpineStore:\n49\t        return SpineStore(self.spine_path)         # fresh read each request (cheap, current)\n50\t\n51\t\n52\tdef _json_bytes(obj) -> bytes:\n53\t    return json.dumps(obj, ensure_ascii=False).encode(\"utf-8\")\n54\t\n55\t\n56\tclass Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):\n57\t    server_version = \"sigil-ui/1.0\"\n58\t    timeout = 30                        # per-connection socket timeout (BLOCK-4: no hung reader)\n59\t    _MAX_BODY = 65536                   # action bodies are tiny; cap to avoid a Content-Length hang\n60\t    _STATIC_OK = frozenset({\"app.js\", \"style.css\"})\n61\t\n62\t    # never log the token (it can ride in ?token= for SSE)\n63\t    def log_message(self, fmt, *args):  # noqa: A003\n64\t        pass\n65\t\n66\t    # --- auth -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n67\t    def _query(self) -> dict:\n68\t        return parse_qs(urlparse(self.path).query)\n69\t\n70\t    def _token_ok(self) -> bool:\n71\t        q = self._query()\n72\t        tok = self.headers.get(\"X-SIGIL-Token\") or (q.get(\"token\", [\"\"])[0])\n73\t        return bool(tok) and hmac.compare_digest(tok, self.server.token)\n74\t\n75\t    def _action_ok(self) -> bool:\n76\t        if self.headers.get(\"Host\", \"\") not in self.server.allowed_hosts:\n77\t            return False                                       # anti DNS-rebinding\n78\t        o = self.headers.get(\"Origin\") or \"\"\n79\t        ref = self.headers.get(\"Referer\") or \"\"\n80\t        # EXACT-match the Origin (a `startswith` lets `http://127.0.0.1:80.evil.com` slip); a Referer,\n81\t        # if present, must sit under an allowed origin. A cross-origin Origin → refuse.\n82\t        if o and o not in self.server.allowed_origins:\n83\t            return False\n84\t        if ref and not any(ref.startswith(a + \"/\") or ref == a for a in self.server.allowed_origins):\n85\t            return False\n86\t        return self._token_ok()\n87\t\n88\t    # --- response helpers -------------------------------------------------------------------------\n89\t    def _send(self, code: int, body: bytes, ctype=\"application/json\"):\n90\t        self.send_response(code)\n91\t        self.send_header(\"Content-Type\", ctype)\n92\t        self.send_header(\"Content-Length\", str(len(body)))\n93\t        self.send_header(\"Content-Security-Policy\", _CSP)\n94\t        self.send_header(\"X-Content-Type-Options\", \"nosniff\")\n95\t        self.send_header(\"Referrer-Policy\", \"no-referrer\")\n96\t        self.end_headers()\n97\t        try:\n98\t            self.wfile.write(body)\n99\t        except (BrokenPipeError, ConnectionResetError):\n100\t            pass\n101\t\n102\t    def _json(self, obj, code=200):\n103\t        self._send(code, _json_bytes(obj))\n104\t\n105\t    def _deny(self, code=403, msg=\"forbidden\"):\n106\t        self._json({\"error\": msg}, code)\n107\t\n108\t    # --- GET (read plane) -------------------------------------------------------------------------\n109\t    def do_GET(self):\n110\t        path = urlparse(self.path).path\n111\t        if path in (\"/\", \"/index.html\"):\n112\t            return self._serve_index()\n113\t        if path.startswith(\"/static/\"):\n114\t            return self._serve_static(path.rsplit(\"/\", 1)[-1])   # token-free bootstrap assets (no secret)\n115\t        if not path.startswith(\"/api/\"):\n116\t            return self._deny(404, \"not found\")\n117\t        if not self._token_ok():\n118\t            return self._deny(401, \"missing/invalid token\")\n119\t        if path == \"/api/ask\":\n120\t            # /api/ask DISPATCHES a KERNEL subprocess → it gets the FULL action gate, not just token.\n121\t            return self._ask(self._query().get(\"q\", [\"\"])[0]) if self._action_ok() else self._deny(403, \"denied\")\n122\t        if path == \"/api/snapshot\":\n123\t            from ..dashboard import snapshot\n124\t            return self._json(snapshot(self.server.store()))\n125\t        if path.startswith(\"/api/record/\"):\n126\t            return self._record(path.rsplit(\"/\", 1)[-1])\n127\t        if path == \"/api/stream\":\n128\t            return self._sse()\n129\t        if path == \"/api/graph\":\n130\t            return self._graph()\n131\t        if path == \"/api/graph/entity\":\n132\t            return self._graph_entity(self._query().get(\"name\", [\"\"])[0])\n133\t        if path == \"/api/classify\":\n134\t            return self._classify(self._query().get(\"tool\", [\"\"])[0])\n135\t        return self._deny(404, \"unknown endpoint\")\n136\t\n137\t    def _serve_static(self, name):\n138\t        if name not in self._STATIC_OK:\n139\t            return self._deny(404, \"not found\")\n140\t        try:\n141\t            data = (_STATIC / name).read_bytes()\n142\t        except OSError:\n143\t            return self._deny(404, \"not found\")\n144\t        ctype = \"application/javascript\" if name.endswith(\".js\") else \"text/css\"\n145\t        self._send(200, data, ctype=f\"{ctype}; charset=utf-8\")\n146\t\n147\t    def _serve_index(self):\n148\t        try:\n149\t            html = (_STATIC / \"index.html\").read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\")\n150\t        except OSError:\n151\t            return self._deny(500, \"ui missing\")\n152\t        html = html.replace(\"__SIGIL_TOKEN__\", self.server.token)   # embed token for the same-origin page\n153\t        self._send(200, html.encode(\"utf-8\"), ctype=\"text/html; charset=utf-8\")\n154\t\n155\t    def _record(self, raw):\n156\t        try:\n157\t            seq = int(raw)\n158\t        except ValueError:\n159\t            return self._deny(400, \"bad seq\")\n160\t        rec = self.server.store().get(seq)\n161\t        if rec is None:\n162\t            return self._json({\"error\": \"no such record\", \"seq\": seq, \"note\": \"no grounded record — not fabricated\"}, 404)\n163\t        ok, reason = verify_record(rec)                          # re-verify the atom LIVE (prove-don't-guess)\n164\t        self._json({\"seq\": rec.seq, \"kind\": rec.kind, \"source\": rec.source, \"actor\": rec.actor,\n165\t                    \"ts\": rec.ts, \"entry_hash\": rec.entry_hash, \"prev_hash\": rec.prev_hash,\n166\t                    \"payload\": rec.payload, \"integrity_ok\": ok, \"integrity_reason\": reason})\n167\t\n168\t    def _graph(self):\n169\t        try:\n170\t            from ..graph import health\n171\t            self._json({\"health\": health()})\n172\t        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001 — graph may not be built yet\n173\t            self._json({\"error\": \"graph unavailable\", \"note\": str(e)[:200]})\n174\t\n175\t    def _graph_entity(self, name):\n176\t        try:\n177\t            from ..graph import entity\n178\t            self._json(entity(name))\n179\t        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001\n180\t            self._json({\"error\": \"graph unavailable\", \"note\": str(e)[:200]})\n181\t\n182\t    def _classify(self, tool):\n183\t        from ..agents.kernel_classify import KernelClassifier\n184\t        self._json({\"tool\": tool, \"tier\": KernelClassifier().classify(tool).label()})\n185\t\n186\t    def _ask(self, q):\n187\t        from ..voice.dispatch import KernelDispatch\n188\t        self._json({\"q\": q, \"answer\": KernelDispatch().send(q)})\n189\t\n190\t    def _sse(self):\n191\t        self.send_response(200)\n192\t        self.send_header(\"Content-Type\", \"text/event-stream\")\n193\t        self.send_header(\"Cache-Control\", \"no-cache\")\n194\t        self.send_header(\"Content-Security-Policy\", _CSP)\n195\t        self.end_headers()\n196\t        try:\n197\t            since = int(self._query().get(\"since\", [\"-1\"])[0])\n198\t        except (ValueError, TypeError):\n199\t            since = -1                                          # any malformed cursor → from genesis\n200\t        tailer = SpineTailer(self.server.store(), since_seq=since)\n201\t        try:\n202\t            while True:\n203\t                sent = False\n204\t                for ev in tailer.poll():\n205\t                    self.wfile.write(f\"data: {json.dumps(ev, ensure_ascii=False)}\\n\\n\".encode(\"utf-8\"))\n206\t                    sent = True\n207\t                self.wfile.write(b\": hb\\n\\n\")                    # heartbeat / flush\n208\t                self.wfile.flush()\n209\t                if not sent:\n210\t                    time.sleep(0.25)\n211\t        except (BrokenPipeError, ConnectionResetError, OSError):\n212\t            return                                               # client closed — end the stream\n213\t\n214\t    # --- POST (action plane) ----------------------------------------------------------------------\n215\t    def do_POST(self):\n216\t        path = urlparse(self.path).path\n217\t        if path != \"/api/action\":\n218\t            return self._deny(404, \"not found\")\n219\t        if not self._action_ok():\n220\t            return self._deny(403, \"action denied (token / origin / host)\")\n221\t        try:\n222\t            length = int(self.headers.get(\"Content-Length\", \"0\"))\n223\t            if length > self._MAX_BODY:                         # BLOCK-4: cap the body (no CL hang / alloc)\n224\t                return self._deny(413, \"body too large\")\n225\t            body = json.loads(self.rfile.read(length) or b\"{}\")\n226\t            action = str(body.get(\"action\", \"\"))\n227\t            result = _actions.do_action(action, body, store=self.server.store())\n228\t            self._json(result)\n229\t        except (ValueError, KeyError) as e:\n230\t            self._deny(400, f\"bad request: {e}\")\n231\t        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001 — ApprovalError etc. → 400, never 500-leak internals\n232\t            self._deny(400, f\"action failed: {str(e)[:200]}\")\n233\t\n234\t\n235\tdef build_server(*, token: str, port: int = 8733, spine_path=None) -> UIServer:\n236\t    return UIServer((\"127.0.0.1\", port), Handler, token=token,\n237\t                    spine_path=Path(spine_path) if spine_path else SPINE_PATH)\n238\t\n239\t\n240\tdef serve(*, token: str, port: int = 8733, spine_path=None) -> None:\n241\t    srv = build_server(token=token, port=port, spine_path=spine_path)\n242\t    print(f\"  SIGIL cockpit → http://127.0.0.1:{port}/?token={token}\")\n243\t    print(\"  (loopback only; the token gates every request — keep it to yourself)\")\n244\t    try:\n245\t        srv.serve_forever()\n246\t    except KeyboardInterrupt:\n247\t        srv.shutdown()\n248\t"}]},"uuid":"5c62f87a-ba53-4912-a805-174850d938ac","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:56:27.641Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"324796a5-96e5-4cfa-8447-610bda20192a","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

The browser never holds key material: it sends an\n3\tauthenticated REQUEST (\"approve seq N\"), and the SERVER signs with the persisted owner key exactly\n4\tas `cli.cmd_warden`/`cmd_approve` do. No new authority path — this only calls the existing\n5\towner-signed cores (`ApprovalQueue`, `KillSwitch`, `PromotionPolicy`).\"\"\"\n6\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n7\t\n8\tfrom typing import Optional\n9\t\n10\tfrom ..spine.store import SpineStore\n11\t\n12\t# the closed set of gated actions the plane will route (fail-closed: anything else is refused)\n13\tACTIONS = frozenset({\"approve\", \"deny\", \"kill\", \"release\", \"promote\", \"revoke\"})\n14\t\n15\t\n16\tdef do_action(action: str, params: dict, *, store: Optional[SpineStore] = None) -> dict:\n17\t    \"\"\"Perform one owner-signed action. Returns {seq, action, ...} or raises ValueError/ApprovalError.\n18\t    The owner key is the persisted identity (auto-created once) — never supplied by the caller.\"\"\"\n19\t    if action not in ACTIONS:\n20\t        raise ValueError(f\"unknown action: {action!r}\")\n21\t    store = store or SpineStore()\n22\t    from ..agents.approvals import ApprovalQueue\n23\t    from ..governor import KillSwitch, PromotionPolicy\n24\t    from ..governor.identity import ensure_owner_keypair\n25\t    owner = ensure_owner_keypair()\n26\t    reason = str(params.get(\"reason\", \"\"))[:200]\n27\t\n28\t    if action in (\"approve\", \"deny\"):\n29\t        seq = int(params[\"seq\"])\n30\t        q = ApprovalQueue(store, owner_key=owner)\n31\t        out = q.approve(seq, reason=reason) if action == \"approve\" else q.deny(seq, reason=reason)\n32\t        return {\"ok\": True, \"action\": action, \"target_seq\": seq, \"recorded_seq\": out}\n33\t    if action in (\"kill\", \"release\"):\n34\t        ks = KillSwitch(store, owner_key=owner)\n35\t        out = ks.engage(reason=reason) if action == \"kill\" else ks.release(reason=reason)\n36\t        return {\"ok\": True, \"action\": action, \"recorded_seq\": out}\n37\t    if action in (\"promote\", \"revoke\"):\n38\t        pp = PromotionPolicy(store, owner_key=owner)\n39\t        agent, scope = str(params[\"agent\"]), str(params.get(\"scope\", \"*\"))\n40\t        out = pp.grant(agent, scope) if action == \"promote\" else pp.revoke(agent, scope)\n41\t        return {\"ok\": out is not None, \"action\": action, \"agent\": agent, \"scope\": scope, \"recorded_seq\": out}\n42\t    raise ValueError(f\"unhandled action: {action!r}\")   # unreachable (ACTIONS-gated)\n43\t"}]},"uuid":"1980a381-891f-4f06-b486-c847a15606fa","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:56:28.196Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"040a0ad0-f6ed-4cf9-851f-277d1e0a4f37","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"720c21a9-e960-4487-b8d9-d2899a7c8c72","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"0268ad75-d9be-4c45-a80f-a1298654d281","agentId":"a1dbf50fd7cad1ed9","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_0194k5hr1wSzrP1ryKoqs9eF","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Voice → KERNEL dispatch. Recognized text is routed through the Phase-1 KERNEL (`sigil-kernel\n2\task`), so a voice request crosses the SAME T0 router + WARDEN gate + signed action log as any\n3\tother — voice is just another interface onto the one authorized path. The spoken response is the\n4\tKERNEL's answer body (the bracketed [T0…]/[WARDEN…] status lines are stripped).\"\"\"\n5\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n6\t\n7\timport subprocess\n8\tfrom pathlib import Path\n9\t\n10\t\n11\tdef _default_bin() -> str:\n12\t    \"\"\"Resolve the kernel binary portably (WS-D D7): an explicit env override, then package-relative\n13\t    (the repo the package lives in), then the legacy dev path, then PATH.\"\"\"\n14\t    import os\n15\t    exe = \"sigil-kernel.exe\" if os.name == \"nt\" else \"sigil-kernel\"\n16\t    env = os.environ.get(\"SIGIL_KERNEL_BIN\")\n17\t    if env and Path(env).exists():\n18\t        return env\n19\t    # sigil/voice/dispatch.py → repo root is three parents up (sigil/<pkg>/voice/..)\n20\t    repo_root = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]\n21\t    for base in (repo_root, Path(\"/home/kali/sigil\")):\n22\t        for p in (f\"kernel/target/release/{exe}\", f\"kernel/target/debug/{exe}\"):\n23\t            if (base / p).exists():\n24\t                return str(base / p)\n25\t    return exe\n26\t\n27\t\n28\tclass KernelDispatch:\n29\t    def __init__(self, kernel_bin: str | None = None, timeout: int = 60):\n30\t        self.kernel_bin = kernel_bin or _default_bin()\n31\t        self.timeout = timeout\n32\t\n33\t    # exact KERNEL status-line prefixes to strip (so a legitimate answer line that merely starts\n34\t    # with '[' is NOT dropped, and a BLOCKED/error is NOT masked as an answer).\n35\t    _STATUS = (\"[t0\", \"[warden\", \"[blocked\", \"[direct\", \"[dispatch\")\n36\t\n37\t    def send(self, text: str) -> str:\n38\t        text = (text or \"\").strip()\n39\t        if not text:\n40\t            return \"I didn't catch that.\"\n41\t        try:\n42\t            proc = subprocess.run([self.kernel_bin, \"ask\", text],\n43\t                                  capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=self.timeout)\n44\t        except (subprocess.SubprocessError, OSError) as e:\n45\t            return f\"the kernel is unavailable ({e})\"\n46\t        if proc.returncode != 0:\n47\t            tail = (proc.stderr or proc.stdout).strip().splitlines()\n48\t            return f\"that request was refused or errored: {tail[-1][:200]}\" if tail else \"that request was refused.\"\n49\t        if any(ln.lstrip().lower().startswith(\"[blocked\") for ln in proc.stdout.splitlines()):\n50\t            return \"that action needs your approval — it wasn't run.\"\n51\t        body = [ln.strip() for ln in proc.stdout.splitlines()\n52\t                if ln.strip() and not any(ln.lstrip().lower().startswith(s) for s in self._STATUS)]\n53\t        answer = \" \".join(body).strip()\n54\t        return answer[:600] if answer else \"there's no answer for that in memory yet.\"\n55\t"}]},"uuid":"f1f0c7b2-f3f2-4312-88e4-87515a5eb835","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:56:28.828Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"720c21a9-e960-4487-b8d9-d2899a7c8c72","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"f260fe66-98fd-4d80-9aeb-e800a7df2e26","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"0268ad75-d9be-4c45-a80f-a1298654d281","agentId":"a1dbf50fd7cad1ed9","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01S9stCg8F5kxoTVLp96f2dg","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"use strict\";\n2\t// External, CSP-'self' script (no inline anything). The token is injected into the page as a data\n3\t// attribute on <body> (unreadable cross-origin); this reads it and uses it for every request.\n4\tconst TOKEN = document.body.dataset.token || \"\";\n5\tconst H = { headers: { \"X-SIGIL-Token\": TOKEN } };\n6\tconst $ = (s) => document.querySelector(s);\n7\tconst esc = (s) => String(s ?? \"\").replace(/[&<>]/g, (c) => ({ \"&\": \"&amp;\", \"<\": \"&lt;\", \">\": \"&gt;\" }[c]));\n8\tconst api = (p) => fetch(p, H).then((r) => r.json());\n9\t\n10\tasync function refresh() {\n11\t  const s = await api(\"/api/snapshot\");\n12\t  const k = $(\"#kill\");\n13\t  k.textContent = \"kill: \" + s.kill_switch;\n14\t  k.className = s.kill_switch === \"ENGAGED\" ? \"kill-on\" : \"kill-off\";\n15\t  const q = $(\"#queue\");\n16\t  if (!s.pending_approvals || !s.pending_approvals.length) {\n17\t    q.innerHTML = '<span class=\"empty\">nothing awaiting approval</span>';\n18\t  } else {\n19\t    q.innerHTML = s.pending_approvals.map((a) => `<div class=\"item\">\n20\t      <div class=\"meta\">seq ${a.seq} · ${esc(a.tier)} · ${esc(a.kind)} · ${esc(a.agent || \"\")}</div>\n21\t      <div class=\"subj\">${esc(a.subject || \"(no subject)\")}</div>\n22\t      <button class=\"approve\" data-act=\"approve\" data-seq=\"${a.seq}\">Approve</button>\n23\t      <button class=\"deny\" data-act=\"deny\" data-seq=\"${a.seq}\">Deny</button></div>`).join(\"\");\n24\t  }\n25\t  $(\"#activity\").innerHTML = kv(s.recent_by_agent) || '<span class=\"empty\">quiet</span>';\n26\t  $(\"#budget\").innerHTML = Object.entries(s.budget_today || {})\n27\t    .map(([a, v]) => `<b>${esc(a)}</b><span>${v.actions} actions · ${v.interrupts} interrupts</span>`).join(\"\")\n28\t    || '<span class=\"empty\">none</span>';\n29\t  const l = s.ingest_lag || {};\n30\t  $(\"#lag\").innerHTML = `<b>head</b><span>${l.head_seq}</span><b>since checkpoint</b><span>${l.records_since_checkpoint}</span>`;\n31\t}\n32\tconst kv = (o) => Object.entries(o || {}).map(([a, n]) => `<b>${esc(a)}</b><span>${n}</span>`).join(\"\");\n33\t\n34\tasync function act(action, seq) {\n35\t  const r = await fetch(\"/api/action\", {\n36\t    method: \"POST\",\n37\t    headers: { \"X-SIGIL-Token\": TOKEN, \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\" },\n38\t    body: JSON.stringify({ action, seq }),\n39\t  });\n40\t  const j = await r.json();\n41\t  if (!r.ok) alert(\"action refused: \" + (j.error || r.status));\n42\t  refresh();\n43\t}\n44\t\n45\tasync function showRecord(seq) {\n46\t  const r = await api(\"/api/record/\" + seq);\n47\t  const badge = r.integrity_ok === false\n48\t    ? '<span class=\"chip broken\">INTEGRITY BROKEN</span>'\n49\t    : '<span class=\"chip ok\">verified</span>';\n50\t  $(\"#detail-body\").innerHTML =\n51\t    `<div class=\"prov\">record <b>seq ${r.seq}</b> · ${esc(r.kind)} · entry_hash <b>${esc((r.entry_hash || \"\").slice(0, 24))}</b> ${badge}</div>\n52\t     <div class=\"prov\">${esc(r.integrity_reason || \"\")}</div><pre>${esc(JSON.stringify(r.payload, null, 2))}</pre>`;\n53\t  $(\"#detail\").style.display = \"block\";\n54\t}\n55\t\n56\tfunction feedRow(ev) {\n57\t  const anc = ev.integrity_ok === false ? '<span class=\"chip broken\">broken</span>'\n58\t    : ev.anchored ? '<span class=\"chip anchored\">anchored</span>' : '<span class=\"chip tail\">tail</span>';\n59\t  const el = document.createElement(\"div\");\n60\t  el.className = \"row\";\n61\t  el.dataset.seq = ev.seq;\n62\t  el.innerHTML = `<span class=\"seq\">#${ev.seq}</span><span class=\"kind\">${esc(ev.kind)}</span><span class=\"actor\">${esc(ev.actor)}</span><span class=\"txt\">${esc((ev.text || \"\").slice(0, 140))}</span>${anc}`;\n63\t  return el;\n64\t}\n65\tfunction startStream() {\n66\t  const es = new EventSource(\"/api/stream?token=\" + encodeURIComponent(TOKEN));\n67\t  const feed = $(\"#feed\");\n68\t  es.onmessage = (e) => {\n69\t    const ev = JSON.parse(e.data);\n70\t    feed.prepend(feedRow(ev));\n71\t    while (feed.children.length > 300) feed.lastChild.remove();\n72\t  };\n73\t}\n74\t\n75\t// event delegation (no inline handlers — CSP-safe)\n76\tdocument.addEventListener(\"click\", (e) => {\n77\t  const btn = e.target.closest(\"button[data-act]\");\n78\t  if (btn) return act(btn.dataset.act, Number(btn.dataset.seq));\n79\t  const row = e.target.closest(\".row[data-seq]\");\n80\t  if (row) return showRecord(Number(row.dataset.seq));\n81\t  if (e.target.closest(\"#detail-close\")) $(\"#detail\").style.display = \"none\";\n82\t});\n83\t\n84\trefresh();\n85\tsetInterval(refresh, 4000);\n86\tstartStream();\n87\t"}]},"uuid":"f5163009-be89-4b26-a39c-af011ec8d237","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:56:33.180Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"f260fe66-98fd-4d80-9aeb-e800a7df2e26","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"d89c9c12-0241-4f48-a941-3e845ed2d9fa","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"0268ad75-d9be-4c45-a80f-a1298654d281","agentId":"a1dbf50fd7cad1ed9","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01CF3HDLvtdUaH7iMorq7tnw","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t<!doctype html>\n2\t<html lang=\"en\">\n3\t<head>\n4\t<meta charset=\"utf-8\">\n5\t<meta name=\"viewport\" content=\"width=device-width, initial-scale=1\">\n6\t<title>SIGIL · cockpit</title>\n7\t<link rel=\"stylesheet\" href=\"/static/style.css\">\n8\t</head>\n9\t<body data-token=\"__SIGIL_TOKEN__\">\n10\t<header>\n11\t  <h1>◆ SIGIL</h1><span class=\"sub\">glass cockpit · loopback · provenance-first</span>\n12\t  <span id=\"kill\" class=\"kill-off\">…</span>\n13\t</header>\n14\t<main>\n15\t  <section class=\"panel\"><h2>Approval queue</h2><div id=\"queue\" class=\"q\"><span class=\"empty\">loading…</span></div></section>\n16\t  <section class=\"panel\"><h2>Agent activity (recent)</h2><div id=\"activity\" class=\"kv\"></div>\n17\t    <h2 class=\"mt\">Budget today</h2><div id=\"budget\" class=\"kv\"></div>\n18\t    <h2 class=\"mt\">Ingest lag</h2><div id=\"lag\" class=\"kv\"></div></section>\n19\t  <section class=\"panel feed\"><h2>Live event stream · click any atom to verify its provenance</h2>\n20\t    <div id=\"feed\"></div></section>\n21\t</main>\n22\t<div id=\"detail\"><span id=\"detail-close\" class=\"close\">✕ close</span>\n23\t  <div id=\"detail-body\"></div></div>\n24\t<script src=\"/static/app.js\"></script>\n25\t</body>\n26\t</html>\n27\t"}]},"uuid":"795a662c-b6d4-41b5-907c-0f6ff698286b","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:56:33.598Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"d89c9c12-0241-4f48-a941-3e845ed2d9fa","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"795a662c-b6d4-41b5-907c-0f6ff698286b","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a1dbf50fd7cad1ed9","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdAMBUvbRDiLvcj8ugdDb","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01V2AG1FCvtB73KfspwYwecD","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/home/kali/sigil/sigil/agents/approvals.py"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":7787,"cache_read_input_tokens":15003,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":7787,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdAMBTv52jYkME8moQqe4","attributionAgent":"Explore","type":"assistant","uuid":"445d04d0-5d76-49d9-b7bc-56f3c87675ae","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:56:34.084Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"445d04d0-5d76-49d9-b7bc-56f3c87675ae","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"0268ad75-d9be-4c45-a80f-a1298654d281","agentId":"a1dbf50fd7cad1ed9","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01V2AG1FCvtB73KfspwYwecD","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Approval queue (SIGIL §5, §9.3 mobile bridge) — the human gate for A2/A3 proposals. An agent\n2\tQUEUES a proposal (governor → QUEUE); the owner APPROVES or DENIES it here. Authentication is an\n3\tEd25519 signature over the canonical (target, decision, approver) by the OWNER key.\n4\t\n5\tRED-PEN HARDENING (Phase 6):\n6\t • The trusted pubkey is the PERSISTED owner identity — NOT the key supplied to the queue, so an\n7\t   attacker cannot self-certify by bringing their own key (RP-APPROVAL-2). approve/deny require the\n8\t   supplied signing key to BE the trusted owner key.\n9\t • `pending()` treats a queued item as resolved ONLY when a superseding approval VERIFIES against the\n10\t   trusted owner key, and keys resolution off the SIGNED target_seq (not the raw supersedes_id) — so\n11\t   an unsigned/forged approval can't silently drop an item (RP-1/RP-APPROVAL-1), and a genuine\n12\t   approval of one item can't be replayed onto another (RP-APPROVAL-4). `verify_approval` is thereby\n13\t   on the enforcement path (RP-APPROVAL-3).\n14\t\n15\tThis records the DECISION; it executes no external effect (SIGIL has no send/deploy path). The\n16\ttransport (Telegram/WhatsApp over WireGuard, §9.3) is a seam over this authenticated core.\"\"\"\n17\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n18\t\n19\tfrom typing import List, Optional\n20\t\n21\tfrom ..reuse import canonical_json, sha256_hex, sign, verify_one\n22\tfrom ..spine.store import SpineStore\n23\t\n24\tSIGNAL = \"governor.approval\"\n25\t_QUEUED_STATUS = \"awaiting-approval\"\n26\t\n27\t\n28\tdef _approval_message(target_seq, decision: str, approver: str) -> bytes:\n29\t    m = canonical_json({\"target\": target_seq, \"decision\": decision, \"approver\": approver})\n30\t    return m if isinstance(m, bytes) else m.encode()\n31\t\n32\t\n33\tdef verify_approval(record, trusted_pubkey_b64: Optional[str], *, extra_pubkeys=None) -> bool:\n34\t    \"\"\"True iff the approval carries a valid signature over its (target, decision, approver) by the\n35\t    OWNER key OR by a currently-authorized DEVICE key (`extra_pubkeys`). The claimed `pubkey` must be\n36\t    in the allowed set — which is composed ONLY of owner-minted keys (the owner identity + the\n37\t    owner-signed device-authorization ledger, see `sigil.mesh`) — so a key the requester merely\n38\t    presents is never trusted (RP-APPROVAL-2). The signed `target_seq` still binds (RP-APPROVAL-4).\n39\t    Unsigned / not-in-allowed-set / no-trusted-key → False (fail-closed).\"\"\"\n40\t    p = record.payload if hasattr(record, \"payload\") else record\n41\t    sig, pub = p.get(\"sig\"), p.get(\"pubkey\")\n42\t    allowed = set()\n43\t    if trusted_pubkey_b64:\n44\t        allowed.add(trusted_pubkey_b64)\n45\t    if extra_pubkeys:\n46\t        allowed.update(extra_pubkeys)\n47\t    if not sig or not pub or pub not in allowed:\n48\t        return False\n49\t    msg = _approval_message(p.get(\"target_seq\"), p.get(\"approval\"), p.get(\"approver\"))\n50\t    return verify_one(pub, msg, sig)                # verify against the (owner-minted) claimed key\n51\t\n52\t\n53\tdef pending(store: SpineStore, trusted_pubkey_b64: Optional[str] = None, *, extra_pubkeys=None) -> List:\n54\t    \"\"\"Queued proposals with NO valid approval yet, oldest first. Resolution requires a VERIFIED\n55\t    owner/authorized-device approval; an unsigned/forged/unauthorized approval leaves the item\n56\t    pending (fail-closed).\"\"\"\n57\t    if trusted_pubkey_b64 is None:\n58\t        from ..governor.identity import owner_pubkey\n59\t        trusted_pubkey_b64 = owner_pubkey()\n60\t    resolved = set()\n61\t    queued = {}\n62\t    for r in store.iter_records():\n63\t        p = r.payload\n64\t        if p.get(\"signal\") == SIGNAL and verify_approval(r, trusted_pubkey_b64, extra_pubkeys=extra_pubkeys):\n65\t            resolved.add(p.get(\"target_seq\"))       # honor the SIGNED target, not raw supersedes_id\n66\t        if p.get(\"decision\") == \"queued\" and p.get(\"status\") == _QUEUED_STATUS:\n67\t            queued[r.seq] = r\n68\t    return [r for seq, r in sorted(queued.items()) if seq not in resolved]\n69\t\n70\t\n71\tclass ApprovalError(Exception):\n72\t    pass\n73\t\n74\t\n75\tclass ApprovalQueue:\n76\t    def __init__(self, store: Optional[SpineStore] = None, *, owner_key=None,\n77\t                 trusted_pubkey_b64: Optional[str] = None):\n78\t        from ..governor.identity import owner_keypair, owner_pubkey\n79\t        self.store = store or SpineStore()\n80\t        self.owner_key = owner_key if owner_key is not None else owner_keypair()\n81\t        # PINNED to the persisted owner identity — never defaulted to the supplied key (RP-APPROVAL-2)\n82\t        self.trusted_pubkey_b64 = trusted_pubkey_b64 if trusted_pubkey_b64 is not None else owner_pubkey()\n83\t\n84\t    def _target(self, seq: int):\n85\t        for r in pending(self.store, self.trusted_pubkey_b64):\n86\t            if r.seq == seq:\n87\t                return r\n88\t        raise ApprovalError(f\"seq {seq} is not a pending approval (already decided, or not queued)\")\n89\t\n90\t    def _decide(self, seq: int, decision: str, *, approver: str, reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n91\t        target = self._target(seq)\n92\t        # the signing key MUST be the trusted owner key — not merely \"some key present\" (RP-APPROVAL-2).\n93\t        if (self.owner_key is None or self.trusted_pubkey_b64 is None\n94\t                or self.owner_key.public_key_b64 != self.trusted_pubkey_b64):\n95\t            raise ApprovalError(\"approval requires the OWNER's signing key (missing or not the trusted key)\")\n96\t        msg = _approval_message(seq, decision, approver)\n97\t        sig = sign(self.owner_key.private_key_b64, msg)\n98\t        payload = {\"signal\": SIGNAL, \"approval\": decision, \"target_seq\": seq, \"target_kind\": target.kind,\n99\t                   \"target_tier\": target.payload.get(\"tier\"), \"approver\": approver, \"reason\": reason,\n100\t                   \"pubkey\": self.trusted_pubkey_b64, \"sig\": sig, \"msg_digest\": sha256_hex(msg),\n101\t                   \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n102\t        return self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"governor\", actor=\"OWNER\",\n103\t                                 payload=payload, supersedes_id=seq)\n104\t\n105\t    def approve(self, seq: int, *, approver: str = \"owner\", reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n106\t        return self._decide(seq, \"approved\", approver=approver, reason=reason)\n107\t\n108\t    def deny(self, seq: int, *, approver: str = \"owner\", reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n109\t        return self._decide(seq, \"denied\", approver=approver, reason=reason)\n110\t"}]},"uuid":"ddb173ad-b9d9-48f6-be91-9944863c8e7c","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:56:34.091Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"445d04d0-5d76-49d9-b7bc-56f3c87675ae","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"ddb173ad-b9d9-48f6-be91-9944863c8e7c","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a1dbf50fd7cad1ed9","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdAMBUvbRDiLvcj8ugdDb","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01J36JBLeZnDVY2LALZtVQHc","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/home/kali/sigil/sigil/gesture/session.py"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":7787,"cache_read_input_tokens":15003,"output_tokens":384,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":7787},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":1,"output_tokens":384,"cache_read_input_tokens":15003,"cache_creation_input_tokens":7787,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":7787,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdAMBTv52jYkME8moQqe4","attributionAgent":"Explore","type":"assistant","uuid":"af6c3798-ca76-4353-b2fc-416db0be8b00","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:56:34.215Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"af6c3798-ca76-4353-b2fc-416db0be8b00","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"0268ad75-d9be-4c45-a80f-a1298654d281","agentId":"a1dbf50fd7cad1ed9","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01J36JBLeZnDVY2LALZtVQHc","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"SessionGate (Phase 8, WS-F, THE KEYSTONE) — the ONLY authority that injects input, and only inside\n2\tan owner-armed session. Two enforcement layers so a gesture can NEVER trigger an unintended action:\n3\t\n4\t  Layer 1 — the SESSION: `arm()` REQUIRES the owner key (it cannot be armed without the owner\n5\t  identity) and appends a signed `gesture.session_armed` record — the tamper-evident AUDIT proof-of-\n6\t  authorization, indicator-lit. The INJECTION gate itself is the live in-memory session, held ONLY by\n7\t  the owner's own running daemon process; it is BOUNDED by a TTL and ended by disarm / hand-loss /\n8\t  expiry. Injection is REFUSED with no live session.\n9\t\n10\t  Layer 2 — per intent, the tier is DERIVED from the WARDEN oracle (`hid.*` names): A1 (pointer\n11\t  move/click/scroll/drag) injects within the session; A2 (type/combo/app.launch) is QUEUED for a\n12\t  verified owner/device approval bound to `sha256(session_id|tool|args)` and NEVER auto-injected — so\n13\t  a gesture alone can never type a password or launch an app.\n14\t\n15\tOnly arm/disarm + DISCRETE actions (click/scroll/drag injected; type/launch queued) hit the spine —\n16\tper-frame pointer MOVES are telemetry (30 FPS records would DoS the append-only log).\"\"\"\n17\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n18\t\n19\timport time\n20\timport uuid\n21\tfrom dataclasses import dataclass\n22\tfrom typing import Optional\n23\t\n24\tfrom ..agents.base import Tier\n25\tfrom ..reuse import sha256_hex\n26\tfrom .components import InputBackend\n27\tfrom .types import GestureIntent\n28\t\n29\tSESSION_ARMED = \"gesture.session_armed\"\n30\tSESSION_DISARMED = \"gesture.session_disarmed\"\n31\tACTION_SIGNAL = \"gesture.action\"\n32\t\n33\tINTENT_TOOL = {\n34\t    \"move\": \"hid.pointer.move\", \"click\": \"hid.pointer.click\",\n35\t    \"scroll_left\": \"hid.pointer.scroll\", \"scroll_right\": \"hid.pointer.scroll\",\n36\t    \"drag\": \"hid.pointer.drag\", \"type\": \"hid.type\", \"combo\": \"hid.combo\", \"launch\": \"hid.app.launch\",\n37\t}\n38\t\n39\t\n40\t@dataclass\n41\tclass Session:\n42\t    session_id: str\n43\t    live: bool = True\n44\t    expires_at: float = 0.0            # wallclock deadline — a session is BOUNDED, never indefinite\n45\t\n46\t    def expired(self, now: float) -> bool:\n47\t        return self.expires_at > 0.0 and now >= self.expires_at\n48\t\n49\t\n50\tclass SessionGate:\n51\t    def __init__(self, store, backend: InputBackend, *, classifier=None, owner_key=None):\n52\t        self.store = store\n53\t        self.backend = backend\n54\t        self._classifier = classifier\n55\t        self._owner_key = owner_key\n56\t        self.session: Optional[Session] = None\n57\t\n58\t    def _cls(self):\n59\t        if self._classifier is None:\n60\t            from ..agents.kernel_classify import KernelClassifier\n61\t            self._classifier = KernelClassifier()\n62\t        return self._classifier\n63\t\n64\t    # --- Layer 1: the owner-armed session ---------------------------------------------------------\n65\t    def arm(self, *, owner_key=None, ttl_seconds: float = 1800.0) -> Session:\n66\t        from ..governor.authn import signed_payload\n67\t        from ..governor.identity import owner_keypair\n68\t        ok = owner_key if owner_key is not None else (self._owner_key or owner_keypair())\n69\t        if ok is None:\n70\t            raise RuntimeError(\"cannot arm a gesture session without the owner key (BLOCK-3)\")\n71\t        sid = uuid.uuid4().hex\n72\t        core = {\"signal\": SESSION_ARMED, \"session_id\": sid}\n73\t        self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"gesture\", actor=\"OWNER\",\n74\t                          payload={**signed_payload(core, ok), \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\",\n75\t                                   \"ttl_seconds\": ttl_seconds,\n76\t                                   \"summary\": \"gesture session ARMED (indicator lit — opt-in)\"})\n77\t        self.session = Session(sid, live=True, expires_at=time.time() + ttl_seconds)\n78\t        return self.session\n79\t\n80\t    def disarm(self) -> None:\n81\t        if self.session is not None:\n82\t            self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"gesture\", actor=\"OWNER\",\n83\t                              payload={\"signal\": SESSION_DISARMED, \"session_id\": self.session.session_id,\n84\t                                       \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\", \"summary\": \"gesture session DISARMED\"})\n85\t            self.session.live = False\n86\t        self.session = None\n87\t\n88\t    # --- Layer 2: per-intent tier gate ------------------------------------------------------------\n89\t    def handle(self, intent: GestureIntent) -> dict:\n90\t        \"\"\"Authorize + act on ONE intent. Returns a verdict dict. Injection ONLY inside a live session\n91\t        and ONLY for A1 tools; A2+ queues for approval and injects NOTHING; a danger name → refused.\"\"\"\n92\t        if intent.kind == \"hand_lost\":\n93\t            self.disarm()\n94\t            return {\"injected\": False, \"reason\": \"hand lost — session disarmed\"}\n95\t        if self.session is None or not self.session.live:\n96\t            return {\"injected\": False, \"reason\": \"no armed session — injection refused\"}\n97\t        if self.session.expired(time.time()):             # BLOCK-2: a session is bounded, never indefinite\n98\t            self.disarm()\n99\t            return {\"injected\": False, \"reason\": \"session expired — disarmed\"}\n100\t        tool = INTENT_TOOL.get(intent.kind)\n101\t        if tool is None:\n102\t            return {\"injected\": False, \"reason\": f\"unknown intent {intent.kind}\"}\n103\t        tier = self._cls().classify(tool)                 # DERIVED, fail-closed A3 on any error\n104\t        args = f\"{intent.dx:.4f},{intent.dy:.4f},{intent.arg}\"\n105\t        if tier <= Tier.A1:\n106\t            self._inject(intent)\n107\t            # BLOCK-1: log the DISCRETE A1 injections that actually take effect (click/scroll/drag);\n108\t            # per-frame `move` is telemetry (logging it at 30 FPS would DoS the append-only spine).\n109\t            if intent.kind != \"move\":\n110\t                self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"gesture\", actor=\"OWNER\",\n111\t                                  payload={\"signal\": ACTION_SIGNAL, \"decision\": \"auto\", \"tier\": tier.label(),\n112\t                                           \"tool\": tool, \"session_id\": self.session.session_id,\n113\t                                           \"summary\": f\"gesture {tool} injected\"})\n114\t            return {\"injected\": True, \"tier\": tier.label(), \"tool\": tool}\n115\t        # A2/A3 → QUEUE an approval bound to this exact action; inject NOTHING\n116\t        token = sha256_hex(f\"{self.session.session_id}|{tool}|{args}\".encode(\"utf-8\"))\n117\t        seq = self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"gesture\", actor=\"PERCEPTION\",\n118\t                                payload={\"signal\": ACTION_SIGNAL, \"decision\": \"queued\",\n119\t                                         \"status\": \"awaiting-approval\", \"tier\": tier.label(),\n120\t                                         \"tool\": tool, \"action_token\": token, \"args\": args,\n121\t                                         \"subject\": f\"gesture action {tool} (awaiting approval)\"})\n122\t        return {\"injected\": False, \"queued\": seq, \"tier\": tier.label(), \"tool\": tool, \"action_token\": token}\n123\t\n124\t    def _inject(self, intent: GestureIntent) -> None:\n125\t        b = self.backend\n126\t        if intent.kind == \"move\" or intent.kind == \"drag\":\n127\t            b.move(intent.dx, intent.dy)\n128\t        elif intent.kind == \"click\":\n129\t            b.click()\n130\t        elif intent.kind == \"scroll_left\":\n131\t            b.scroll(-1, 0)\n132\t        elif intent.kind == \"scroll_right\":\n133\t            b.scroll(1, 0)\n134\t"}]},"uuid":"1e9fb8c8-a6b7-4825-a865-af8f1e4c2ceb","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:56:34.250Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"af6c3798-ca76-4353-b2fc-416db0be8b00","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"3e270865-ece7-4fc2-884c-1af67cba8362","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a1dbf50fd7cad1ed9","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdAMBsa7fpCbzbt4hPc4D","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01EGCirvneAs4V5PKJMsKzB3","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/home/kali/sigil/sigil/agents/actor.py"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":8657,"cache_read_input_tokens":22790,"output_tokens":197,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":8657},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":197,"cache_read_input_tokens":22790,"cache_creation_input_tokens":8657,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":8657,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdAMBriGkbHUYN1kBgdbv","attributionAgent":"Explore","type":"assistant","uuid":"e08b71bb-3b89-4018-ad1a-09c2a4e79554","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:56:39.856Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"e08b71bb-3b89-4018-ad1a-09c2a4e79554","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"0268ad75-d9be-4c45-a80f-a1298654d281","agentId":"a1dbf50fd7cad1ed9","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01EGCirvneAs4V5PKJMsKzB3","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"DELEGATE (Phase 8, WS-G) — the owner-consented identity/account manager + web actor. Manages the\n2\towner's OWN credentials and, with PER-ACTION owner approval, creates accounts / logs in / fills forms /\n3\tsubmits on the owner's behalf. Ceiling A2, and added to NO_PROMOTION_AGENTS — every account/login/\n4\tsubmit/purchase is A3 (explicit, per-action, owner-signed, no promotion; the WARDEN oracle forces this\n5\tfrom the honest tool name). Fuses the Operator transaction (plan → preview-by-reference →\n6\ttoken-bound-approve → re-derive-at-execute → verify) with the egress per-action token.\n7\t\n8\tOffense-free by construction:\n9\t  • NO `as_identity`/impersonate parameter exists — fields resolve ONLY from the owner's OWN vault.\n10\t  • a DETECTED block (best-effort: CAPTCHA / 403 / 429) STOPS and is surfaced as a positive control; the\n11\t    block-check precedes any action, and there is NO browser-escalation code path in the actor at all\n12\t    (HTTP-only), so \"use a browser to beat a block\" is structurally unreachable.\n13\t  • ONE approval authorises exactly ONE action: execute is single-shot per step (a second execute of an\n14\t    already-applied step is refused), so an approval can never be replayed into repeated POSTs.\n15\t  • per-service creation cap (mass creation is out of doctrine), enforced at preview AND re-checked at\n16\t    execute (so a batch previewed before any execute cannot outrun the cap); `ActorScope` origin\n17\t    allowlist (deny-all), re-checked at execute.\n18\t  • credentials resolve from the keyring ONLY at the last instant of execute, into a local var — never a\n19\t    Proposal payload, never logged/journaled. The executable step (incl. any literal values) lives in a\n20\t    0700 journal OFF the append-only spine; the spine binds by service + URL + page hash + a field_binding\n21\t    of NAMES + vault-references + literal-content-hashes + the vault VERSION (a changed URL OR page OR a\n22\t    credential rotation OR a literal edit changes the token → the approval no longer verifies).\"\"\"\n23\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n24\t\n25\timport json\n26\timport os\n27\tfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\n28\tfrom pathlib import Path\n29\tfrom typing import List\n30\t\n31\tfrom ..config import SIGIL_HOME\n32\tfrom ..reuse import sha256_hex\n33\tfrom .actor_gate import ACTION_SIGNAL, action_approved, action_token\n34\tfrom .actor_scope import ActorScope\n35\tfrom .base import Agent, AgentResult, Proposal, Tier\n36\tfrom .web_engine import HttpEngine, detect_block\n37\t\n38\t_ACTOR_HOME = SIGIL_HOME / \"actor\"\n39\t_JOURNAL = _ACTOR_HOME / \"steps\"     # executable steps (WITH any literal values) — off the append-only spine\n40\t\n41\t# honest tool names — the WARDEN oracle forces each to A3 (explicit, per-action, no-promotion)\n42\t_TOOL = {\"navigate\": \"browser.navigate\", \"fill\": \"form.fill\", \"submit\": \"submit.form\",\n43\t         \"account.create\": \"account.create\", \"login\": \"login\", \"purchase\": \"purchase.item\"}\n44\t\n45\t_VAULT_REFS = {\"vault:password\", \"vault:email\", \"vault:username\"}\n46\t\n47\t\n48\tdef _valid_source(src) -> bool:\n49\t    \"\"\"A field value is a known source: an owner-vault reference or a literal. Anything else (unknown\n50\t    vault key, `env:`/`file:` scheme, non-string) is REFUSED at preview — so the owner never approves a\n51\t    field the actor would silently drop, and no unhandled source can reach execute.\"\"\"\n52\t    return isinstance(src, str) and (src in _VAULT_REFS or src.startswith(\"literal:\"))\n53\t\n54\t\n55\tdef _secure(d: Path) -> None:\n56\t    d.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n57\t    try:\n58\t        os.chmod(d, 0o700)\n59\t    except OSError:\n60\t        pass\n61\t\n62\t\n63\tdef _redact_source(src: str) -> str:\n64\t    \"\"\"Spine-safe rendering of a field source: vault references pass through (they are references, not\n65\t    values); a literal is reduced to a content hash (the value is journaled 0700, never on the spine).\"\"\"\n66\t    if src.startswith(\"literal:\"):\n67\t        return \"literal:\" + sha256_hex(src[len(\"literal:\"):].encode(\"utf-8\"))[:16]\n68\t    return src\n69\t\n70\t\n71\t@dataclass(frozen=True)\n72\tclass WebStep:\n73\t    service: str\n74\t    kind: str                                    # navigate|fill|submit|account.create|login|purchase\n75\t    url: str\n76\t    fields: dict = field(default_factory=dict)   # {form_field: \"vault:password\"|\"vault:email\"|\"vault:username\"|\"literal:...\"}\n77\t    # NOTE: there is deliberately NO identity/impersonate field — fields resolve ONLY from the owner's own vault.\n78\t\n79\t\n80\tclass Delegate(Agent):\n81\t    name = \"DELEGATE\"\n82\t    mandate = \"owner-consented identity/account manager + web actor; per-action approved, offense-free\"\n83\t    ceiling = Tier.A2\n84\t\n85\t    def __init__(self, store=None, *, scope: ActorScope, vault=None, engine=None,\n86\t                 classifier=None, trusted_pubkey=None):\n87\t        super().__init__(store)\n88\t        self.scope = scope\n89\t        self._vault = vault\n90\t        self.engine = engine or HttpEngine()\n91\t        self._classifier = classifier\n92\t        self._trusted = trusted_pubkey\n93\t\n94\t    def _vaultobj(self):\n95\t        if self._vault is None:\n96\t            from .vault import CredentialVault\n97\t            self._vault = CredentialVault()\n98\t        return self._vault\n99\t\n100\t    def _cls(self):\n101\t        if self._classifier is None:\n102\t            from .kernel_classify import KernelClassifier\n103\t            self._classifier = KernelClassifier()\n104\t        return self._classifier\n105\t\n106\t    def _tp(self):\n107\t        if self._trusted is None:\n108\t            from ..governor.identity import owner_pubkey\n109\t            self._trusted = owner_pubkey()\n110\t        return self._trusted\n111\t\n112\t    def _field_binding(self, service: str, fields: dict) -> str:\n113\t        \"\"\"A value-free binding: field NAMES + vault-references + literal-CONTENT-HASHES, prefixed with\n114\t        the current vault VERSION when any field references the vault (so a rotation → new token →\n115\t        re-approval). Deterministically reproducible at execute from the journaled fields + vault state.\"\"\"\n116\t        parts, uses_vault = [], False\n117\t        for k in sorted(fields):\n118\t            src = fields[k]\n119\t            if src.startswith(\"vault:\"):\n120\t                uses_vault = True\n121\t            parts.append(f\"{k}={_redact_source(src)}\")\n122\t        binding = \";\".join(parts)\n123\t        if uses_vault:\n124\t            rec = self._vaultobj().get_record(service)\n125\t            binding = f\"vaultv{rec.version if rec else 0}|{binding}\"\n126\t        return binding\n127\t\n128\t    # --- preview: dry-run GET, block-detect, journal off-spine, queue a per-action A3 (redacted) ---\n129\t    def preview(self, steps: List[WebStep]) -> tuple:\n130\t        res = AgentResult(agent=self.name)\n131\t        queued: List[int] = []\n132\t        for step in steps:\n133\t            if not self.scope.origin_allowed(step.url):\n134\t                self.store.append(kind=\"refusal\", source=\"agent\", actor=self.name,\n135\t                                  payload={\"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"refused\", \"requested\": step.url,\n136\t                                           \"reason\": \"target origin is not in the owner's ActorScope\"})\n137\t                res.notes.append(f\"REFUSED {step.url}: out of ActorScope\")\n138\t                continue\n139\t            if step.kind == \"account.create\" and not self.scope.creation_allowed(self.store, step.service, step.url):\n140\t                self.store.append(kind=\"refusal\", source=\"agent\", actor=self.name,\n141\t                                  payload={\"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"refused\", \"service\": step.service,\n142\t                                           \"reason\": \"creation cap for this service/origin (mass account creation is out of doctrine)\"})\n143\t                res.notes.append(f\"REFUSED account.create {step.service}: creation cap\")\n144\t                continue\n145\t            bad = sorted(k for k, v in step.fields.items() if not _valid_source(v))\n146\t            if bad:                                          # fail-closed: no silently-dropped fields\n147\t                self.store.append(kind=\"refusal\", source=\"agent\", actor=self.name,\n148\t                                  payload={\"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"refused\", \"requested\": step.url,\n149\t                                           \"reason\": f\"unknown/invalid field source(s) for: {bad}\"})\n150\t                res.notes.append(f\"REFUSED {step.url}: invalid field source(s) {bad}\")\n151\t                continue\n152\t            pv = self.engine.fetch(step.url)                 # A0 dry-run GET — no mutation\n153\t            block = detect_block(pv.status, pv.html)\n154\t            if block:                                        # STOP + surface — never defeat a block\n155\t                self.store.append(kind=\"finding\", source=\"agent\", actor=self.name,\n156\t                                  payload={\"signal\": \"web.actor.blocked\", \"decision\": \"surfaced\", \"url\": step.url,\n157\t                                           \"reason\": block, \"tier\": \"A0\",\n158\t                                           \"summary\": f\"{step.url} is blocked ({block}) — surfaced, NOT defeated\"})\n159\t                res.notes.append(f\"BLOCKED {step.url}: {block} — surfaced, not defeated (doctrine)\")\n160\t                continue\n161\t            if not pv.ok:                                    # a non-2xx / unreachable page has nothing to act on\n162\t                self.store.append(kind=\"refusal\", source=\"agent\", actor=self.name,\n163\t                                  payload={\"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"refused\", \"requested\": step.url,\n164\t                                           \"reason\": f\"page not retrievable (status {pv.status}) — nothing to act on\"})\n165\t                res.notes.append(f\"REFUSED {step.url}: page not retrievable (status {pv.status})\")\n166\t                continue\n167\t            page_hash = sha256_hex(pv.html.encode(\"utf-8\", \"ignore\"))\n168\t            fb = self._field_binding(step.service, step.fields)\n169\t            token = action_token(step.service, step.kind, step.url, page_hash, fb)\n170\t            _secure(_ACTOR_HOME)\n171\t            _secure(_JOURNAL)\n172\t            # executable step (WITH any literal values) → 0700 journal, keyed by token; NEVER the spine\n173\t            (_JOURNAL / f\"{token}.json\").write_text(json.dumps(\n174\t                {\"service\": step.service, \"kind\": step.kind, \"url\": step.url, \"fields\": step.fields}),\n175\t                encoding=\"utf-8\")\n176\t            tier = self._cls().classify(_TOOL.get(step.kind, \"unknown.op\"))   # → A3 for the mutating kinds\n177\t            r = self._dispatch([Proposal(\"event\", {\n178\t                \"signal\": ACTION_SIGNAL, \"service\": step.service, \"step_kind\": step.kind, \"url\": step.url,\n179\t                \"page_sha256\": page_hash, \"field_binding\": fb, \"action_token\": token,\n180\t                \"journal\": str(_JOURNAL / f\"{token}.json\"),\n181\t                \"fields_redacted\": [{\"name\": k, \"source\": _redact_source(step.fields[k])}\n182\t                                    for k in sorted(step.fields)],   # NAMES + refs/hashes only — NO values\n183\t                \"subject\": f\"{step.kind} on {step.service} (awaiting owner approval)\"}, tier)])\n184\t            seq = r.queued[0][\"seq\"] if r.queued else (r.applied[0] if r.applied else None)\n185\t            if seq is not None:\n186\t                queued.append(seq)\n187\t        return queued, res\n188\t\n189\t    # --- execute: verified approval → re-fetch → block-before-act → re-derive token → last-instant act\n190\t    def execute(self, step_seq: int) -> AgentResult:\n191\t        res = AgentResult(agent=self.name)\n192\t        rec = self.store.get(step_seq)\n193\t        if rec is None or rec.payload.get(\"signal\") != ACTION_SIGNAL:\n194\t            res.notes.append(f\"seq {step_seq} is not a web-actor step\")\n195\t            return res\n196\t        token = rec.payload[\"action_token\"]\n197\t        if not action_approved(self.store, step_seq, token, self._tp()):\n198\t            res.notes.append(f\"step {step_seq} has no verified owner approval — NO request made\")\n199\t            return res\n200\t        # SINGLE-SHOT: one approval authorises exactly ONE action. A step that already produced an applied\n201\t        # record is refused (the append-only approval never expires, so without this guard one signature\n202\t        # would authorise unbounded repeats). Mirrors the Operator's single-shot undo (BLOCK-6).\n203\t        for r in self.store.iter_records(since_seq=step_seq):\n204\t            if (r.payload.get(\"signal\") == ACTION_SIGNAL and r.payload.get(\"status\") == \"applied\"\n205\t                    and r.payload.get(\"target_seq\") == step_seq):\n206\t                res.notes.append(f\"step {step_seq} was already executed (seq {r.seq}) — refusing a replay (per-action)\")\n207\t                return res\n208\t        try:                                                 # executable step lives OFF the spine (0700)\n209\t            j = json.loads(Path(rec.payload[\"journal\"]).read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\n210\t            service, kind, url, fields = j[\"service\"], j[\"kind\"], j[\"url\"], j[\"fields\"]\n211\t        except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError, TypeError):\n212\t            res.notes.append(\"step journal missing/unreadable — cannot execute\")\n213\t            return res\n214\t        if not self.scope.origin_allowed(url):               # re-check scope at execute (belt-and-suspenders)\n215\t            self.store.append(kind=\"refusal\", source=\"agent\", actor=self.name,\n216\t                              payload={\"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"refused\", \"target_seq\": step_seq, \"requested\": url,\n217\t                                       \"reason\": \"the step URL is not in the owner's ActorScope at execute — aborted\"})\n218\t            res.notes.append(f\"ABORTED: {url} left the ActorScope — no action\")\n219\t            return res\n220\t        pv = self.engine.fetch(url)                          # re-fetch (anti-TOCTOU + block-before-act)\n221\t        block = detect_block(pv.status, pv.html)\n222\t        if block:\n223\t            self.store.append(kind=\"finding\", source=\"agent\", actor=self.name,\n224\t                              payload={\"signal\": \"web.actor.blocked\", \"decision\": \"surfaced\", \"url\": url,\n225\t                                       \"reason\": block, \"tier\": \"A0\",\n226\t                                       \"summary\": f\"{url} blocked at execute ({block}) — surfaced, no action\"})\n227\t            res.notes.append(f\"BLOCKED at execute {url}: {block} — surfaced, no action\")\n228\t            return res\n229\t        if not pv.ok:                                        # unreachable/non-2xx at execute → never POST to a dead page\n230\t            self.store.append(kind=\"refusal\", source=\"agent\", actor=self.name,\n231\t                              payload={\"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"refused\", \"target_seq\": step_seq, \"requested\": url,\n232\t                                       \"reason\": f\"page not retrievable at execute (status {pv.status}) — aborted\"})\n233\t            res.notes.append(f\"ABORTED: {url} not retrievable at execute (status {pv.status}) — no action\")\n234\t            return res\n235\t        # RE-DERIVE the token from the CURRENT url + page hash + vault version + journaled fields; a changed\n236\t        # URL OR page OR a credential rotation OR a journal edit → mismatch → abort (never trust a field).\n237\t        fresh = action_token(service, kind, url, sha256_hex(pv.html.encode(\"utf-8\", \"ignore\")),\n238\t                             self._field_binding(service, fields))\n239\t        if fresh != token:\n240\t            self.store.append(kind=\"refusal\", source=\"agent\", actor=self.name,\n241\t                              payload={\"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"refused\", \"target_seq\": step_seq,\n242\t                                       \"reason\": \"url/page/credential/step changed since approval (token mismatch) — aborted\"})\n243\t            res.notes.append(\"ABORTED: the world changed since approval (token mismatch) — no action\")\n244\t            return res\n245\t        # RE-CHECK the creation cap at execute (against committed applied records) so a batch previewed\n246\t        # before any execute cannot outrun cap=1 — the preview check alone counts zero applied.\n247\t        if kind == \"account.create\" and not self.scope.creation_allowed(self.store, service, url):\n248\t            self.store.append(kind=\"refusal\", source=\"agent\", actor=self.name,\n249\t                              payload={\"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"refused\", \"target_seq\": step_seq, \"service\": service,\n250\t                                       \"reason\": \"per-service creation cap reached at execute (mass creation out of doctrine)\"})\n251\t            res.notes.append(f\"REFUSED account.create {service} at execute: creation cap\")\n252\t            return res\n253\t        resolved = self._resolve(service, fields)            # credentials → locals ONLY (never the spine)\n254\t        out = self.engine.act({\"url\": url, \"kind\": kind}, resolved,\n255\t                              pinned_ip=getattr(pv, \"resolved_ip\", \"\") or None)   # bind the POST to the vetted IP\n256\t        seq = self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"agent\", actor=self.name,\n257\t                                payload={\"signal\": ACTION_SIGNAL, \"status\": \"applied\", \"target_seq\": step_seq,\n258\t                                         \"service\": service, \"step_kind\": kind, \"url\": url, \"action_token\": token,\n259\t                                         \"result_ok\": bool(out.get(\"ok\")), \"tier\": \"A1\", \"decision\": \"auto\",\n260\t                                         \"summary\": f\"{kind} on {service} executed (ok={out.get('ok')})\"})\n261\t        res.applied.append(seq)\n262\t        res.notes.append(f\"executed {kind} on {service} (ok={out.get('ok')})\")\n263\t        return res\n264\t\n265\t    def _resolve(self, service: str, fields: dict) -> dict:\n266\t        \"\"\"Resolve field references to VALUES at the last instant — into a local dict, never the spine.\"\"\"\n267\t        out: dict = {}\n268\t        rec = self._vaultobj().get_record(service)\n269\t        for name, src in fields.items():\n270\t            if src == \"vault:password\":\n271\t                out[name] = self._vaultobj().resolve_password(service) or \"\"\n272\t            elif src == \"vault:email\":\n273\t                out[name] = rec.email if rec else \"\"\n274\t            elif src == \"vault:username\":\n275\t                out[name] = rec.username if rec else \"\"\n276\t            elif src.startswith(\"literal:\"):\n277\t                out[name] = src[len(\"literal:\"):]\n278\t        return out\n279\t"}]},"uuid":"035286cb-a74a-49a9-a9a4-bce2761c3adc","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:56:39.884Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"e08b71bb-3b89-4018-ad1a-09c2a4e79554","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
