{"parentUuid":"f6828dad-b2e0-4668-abdd-5f130e2618c9","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"0268ad75-d9be-4c45-a80f-a1298654d281","agentId":"a1dbf50fd7cad1ed9","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01EzGAaXPWxZqECWff7mRbLF","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"SIGIL MCP memory server (Phase 0a: memory.search).\n2\t\n3\tA gated, read-only, loopback stdio server exposing the owner's total recall to any Claude\n4\tsurface. Ports the CRUCIBLE MCP posture (framework/v2/mcp/server.py): a fail-closed tool\n5\tallowlist, no write path, and provenance on every result — each hit is a real, tamper-\n6\tevident spine record (seq + entry_hash) so answers are CITED, and an empty result says so\n7\trather than inviting fabrication (SIGIL doctrine §1.5, prove-don't-guess).\n8\t\"\"\"\n9\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n10\t\n11\tfrom typing import Any\n12\t\n13\tfrom mcp.server.fastmcp import FastMCP\n14\t\n15\tfrom ..config import EMBED_MODEL, QDRANT_URL\n16\tfrom ..reuse import assert_no_offense\n17\tfrom ..spine.checkpoint import verify_checkpoint\n18\tfrom ..spine.store import SpineStore\n19\tfrom ..vectors.index import VectorIndex\n20\t\n21\tassert_no_offense()  # doctrine §12: no engine module may be loaded in a SIGIL process\n22\t\n23\tmcp = FastMCP(\"sigil-memory\")\n24\t_index: VectorIndex | None = None\n25\t\n26\t\n27\tdef _idx() -> VectorIndex:\n28\t    global _index\n29\t    if _index is None:\n30\t        _index = VectorIndex()\n31\t    return _index\n32\t\n33\t\n34\t@mcp.tool()\n35\tdef memory_search(query: str, k: int = 8) -> dict[str, Any]:\n36\t    \"\"\"Search the owner's ENTIRE Claude history — everything they've built, decided, or\n37\t    discussed — and return ranked memory records WITH PROVENANCE so you can cite them.\n38\t\n39\t    Each result is a real, tamper-evident spine record: `seq` + `entry_hash` uniquely\n40\t    identify it, `when`/`session`/`project` locate it. When you answer from these, CITE\n41\t    the seq(s). If `results` is empty, memory genuinely has nothing — say so; do NOT\n42\t    fabricate an answer.\n43\t\n44\t    Args:\n45\t        query: what to recall (natural language).\n46\t        k: max results (default 8).\n47\t    \"\"\"\n48\t    hits, is_grounded = _idx().grounded(query, k=max(1, min(int(k), 25)))\n49\t    if not is_grounded:\n50\t        return {\"results\": [],\n51\t                \"note\": \"No strongly-grounded match in SIGIL memory for this query. Do not fabricate — \"\n52\t                        \"tell the owner memory has nothing solid on this (optionally suggest rephrasing).\"}\n53\t    return {\n54\t        \"results\": [\n55\t            {\n56\t                \"seq\": h[\"seq\"], \"kind\": h[\"kind\"], \"when\": h.get(\"ts\"),\n57\t                \"session\": h.get(\"session_id\"), \"project\": h.get(\"project\"),\n58\t                \"entry_hash\": h.get(\"entry_hash\"), \"score\": round(h[\"score\"], 4),\n59\t                \"text\": h.get(\"text\"),\n60\t            }\n61\t            for h in hits\n62\t        ],\n63\t        \"provenance\": \"Each result is a tamper-evident spine record; cite its `seq` (and `entry_hash`) when you answer.\",\n64\t    }\n65\t\n66\t\n67\t@mcp.tool()\n68\tdef episodic_range(start_seq: int = 0, end_seq: int | None = None,\n69\t                   kind: str | None = None, limit: int = 25) -> dict[str, Any]:\n70\t    \"\"\"Read a CONTIGUOUS window of the owner's episodic spine in seq order — the raw record\n71\t    stream (messages, tool calls, commits, docs), not a semantic search. Use this to\n72\t    reconstruct *what happened* around a known point (e.g. after `memory_search` gives you a\n73\t    seq, read the seqs around it for context), or to page through a session/day.\n74\t\n75\t    Every record is tamper-evident (seq + entry_hash). Cite seqs when you answer.\n76\t\n77\t    Args:\n78\t        start_seq: first seq to return (inclusive, default 0 = beginning).\n79\t        end_seq: last seq (inclusive); default = newest.\n80\t        kind: optional filter (message | tool_call | tool_result | commit | document | session | decision | commitment | brief).\n81\t        limit: max records (default 25, hard cap 200).\n82\t    \"\"\"\n83\t    store = SpineStore()\n84\t    limit = max(1, min(int(limit), 200))\n85\t    hi = (store.next_seq - 1) if end_seq is None else int(end_seq)\n86\t    lo = max(0, int(start_seq))\n87\t    out: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n88\t    for r in store.iter_records(since_seq=lo - 1):  # yields seq >= lo\n89\t        if r.seq > hi:\n90\t            break\n91\t        if kind and r.kind != kind:\n92\t            continue\n93\t        out.append({\n94\t            \"seq\": r.seq, \"kind\": r.kind, \"source\": r.source, \"actor\": r.actor,\n95\t            \"when\": r.ts, \"session\": r.payload.get(\"session_id\"), \"project\": r.payload.get(\"project\"),\n96\t            \"parent_id\": r.parent_id, \"supersedes_id\": r.supersedes_id,\n97\t            \"entry_hash\": r.entry_hash, \"text\": (r.text() or \"\")[:800],\n98\t        })\n99\t        if len(out) >= limit:\n100\t            break\n101\t    return {\"range\": {\"start_seq\": lo, \"end_seq\": hi}, \"kind\": kind, \"count\": len(out),\n102\t            \"records\": out,\n103\t            \"provenance\": \"Each record is a tamper-evident spine entry; cite its `seq` when you answer.\"}\n104\t\n105\t\n106\t@mcp.tool()\n107\tdef ingest_status(verify: bool = True) -> dict[str, Any]:\n108\t    \"\"\"Report the health of SIGIL memory: how much is stored, how much is embedded for\n109\t    semantic recall, and — with `verify` — whether the spine's integrity chain and signed\n110\t    head still hold (tamper-evidence). Use this to tell the owner how current/trustworthy\n111\t    their memory is before relying on it.\n112\t\n113\t    Args:\n114\t        verify: also run the (heavier) integrity + signed-head check (default true).\n115\t    \"\"\"\n116\t    store = SpineStore()\n117\t    vi = _idx()\n118\t    status: dict[str, Any] = {\n119\t        \"spine_records\": store.count(),\n120\t        \"next_seq\": store.next_seq,\n121\t        \"vectors_indexed\": vi.count(),\n122\t        \"last_indexed_seq\": vi.last_indexed_seq(),\n123\t        \"embedding_model\": EMBED_MODEL,\n124\t        \"qdrant_mode\": \"server\" if QDRANT_URL else \"local-embedded\",\n125\t    }\n126\t    if verify:\n127\t        ok, msg = store.verify()\n128\t        hok, hmsg = verify_checkpoint(store)\n129\t        status[\"chain\"] = {\"ok\": ok, \"detail\": msg}\n130\t        status[\"signed_head\"] = {\"ok\": hok, \"detail\": hmsg}\n131\t    return status\n132\t\n133\t\n134\t@mcp.tool()\n135\tdef graph_entity(name: str) -> dict[str, Any]:\n136\t    \"\"\"Look up a structural entity in the owner's memory graph and return it WITH its\n137\t    neighbours and spine anchors. Resolves `name` as a Project, then a Session id, a Commit\n138\t    hash prefix, or a Document path substring. Use this for a structured overview — e.g.\n139\t    `graph_entity(\"PENTEST-main\")` returns the project's sessions, commits, and documents.\n140\t\n141\t    The graph is a deterministic, rebuilt view of the spine; every node cites the spine\n142\t    `anchor_seq` that minted it, so you can `episodic_range` around it for detail.\n143\t    \"\"\"\n144\t    from ..graph import entity as _entity\n145\t    try:\n146\t        return _entity(name)\n147\t    except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001 — graph may be unbuilt; report, don't crash the tool\n148\t        return {\"error\": str(e), \"hint\": \"the graph may not be built yet\"}\n149\t\n150\t\n151\t@mcp.tool()\n152\tdef graph_query(cypher: str, limit: int = 50) -> dict[str, Any]:\n153\t    \"\"\"Run a READ-ONLY Cypher query over the owner's memory graph (a deterministic view of\n154\t    the spine) and return columns + rows. Writes/DDL are refused.\n155\t\n156\t    Node labels: `Project(name, sessions, commits, documents, messages)`,\n157\t    `Session(id, title, project, messages, first_seq, last_seq, anchor_seq)`,\n158\t    `Document(path, title, project, chunks)`, `Commit(hash, repo, subject, author, date)`.\n159\t    Edges (all point at a Project): `IN_PROJECT_S` (Session), `IN_PROJECT_D` (Document),\n160\t    `IN_PROJECT_C` (Commit). Example: `MATCH (c:Commit) RETURN c.subject, c.date ORDER BY c.anchor_seq DESC`.\n161\t    \"\"\"\n162\t    from ..graph import query as _query\n163\t    try:\n164\t        return _query(cypher, limit=limit)\n165\t    except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001\n166\t        return {\"error\": str(e), \"hint\": \"the graph may not be built yet\"}\n167\t\n168\t\n169\t@mcp.tool()\n170\tdef threads_open(limit: int = 25) -> dict[str, Any]:\n171\t    \"\"\"List the owner's OPEN loops — current, grounded decisions and commitments that nothing\n172\t    later has superseded — most-stale first, each cited by spine seq. Use this to answer\n173\t    \"what's still open / unresolved for me?\". Only facts the consolidation gate GROUNDED\n174\t    appear here; if empty, either nothing is open or the consolidation pass hasn't run.\n175\t    \"\"\"\n176\t    from ..consolidate import open_threads\n177\t    return {\"threads\": open_threads(SpineStore(), limit=max(1, min(int(limit), 100))),\n178\t            \"provenance\": \"each thread is a grounded consolidation record; cite its `seq` (and `source_seqs`).\"}\n179\t\n180\t\n181\t@mcp.tool()\n182\tdef commitments_due(within_days: int | None = None, limit: int = 50) -> dict[str, Any]:\n183\t    \"\"\"Return the owner's commitment ledger — grounded promises that carry a due date,\n184\t    earliest-due first. `within_days` (optional) limits to those due within that many days\n185\t    from now. Each is cited by spine seq. Answers \"what have I promised, and by when?\".\n186\t    \"\"\"\n187\t    from ..consolidate import due_commitments\n188\t    before = None\n189\t    if within_days is not None:\n190\t        from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone\n191\t        before = (datetime.now(timezone.utc) + timedelta(days=int(within_days))).isoformat()\n192\t    return {\"commitments\": due_commitments(SpineStore(), before_iso=before, limit=max(1, min(int(limit), 200))),\n193\t            \"provenance\": \"each commitment is a grounded consolidation record; cite its `seq`.\"}\n194\t\n195\t\n196\t@mcp.tool()\n197\tdef contradictions_pending(limit: int = 25) -> dict[str, Any]:\n198\t    \"\"\"Surface unresolved self-contradictions — subjects on which the owner has more than one\n199\t    live decision. These are FLAGGED for review, never auto-resolved: each names the\n200\t    conflicting decision seqs. Answers \"where have I contradicted myself?\".\n201\t    \"\"\"\n202\t    from ..consolidate import pending_contradictions\n203\t    return {\"contradictions\": pending_contradictions(SpineStore(), limit=max(1, min(int(limit), 100))),\n204\t            \"provenance\": \"each is a consolidation contradiction record; the conflicting decisions are cited by seq.\"}\n205\t\n206\t\n207\tdef run() -> None:\n208\t    mcp.run()  # stdio transport\n209\t\n210\t\n211\tif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n212\t    run()\n213\t"}]},"uuid":"235cee71-91a6-42ae-8211-36b922dbe940","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:56:39.937Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"f6828dad-b2e0-4668-abdd-5f130e2618c9","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

self.store.append(kind=\"refusal\", source=\"agent\", actor=self.name,\n198\t                              payload={\"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"refused\", \"subject\": subject,\n199\t f:\n314\t                    f.write(new)\n315\t                os.replace(tmp, rp)                                  # atomic\n316\t            except OSError as e:\n317\t                Path(tmp).unlink(missing_ok=True)\n318\t                return False, f\"write failed: {e}\", None\n319\t            if pre_mode is not None:\n320\t                try:\n321\t {\"op\": \"restore\" if pre is not None else \"delete-created\", \"path\": str(rp),\n327\t                   \"pre\": str(jdir / f\"{i}.pre\") if pre is not None else None, \"pre_mode\": Returns (restored, skip_reason). `verify_unchanged` (undo) refuses\n346\t        to clobber a file the owner changed since (hash-bind), and re-resolves the path through scope.\"\"\"\n347\t        path = inv.get(\"path\")\n348\t        if verify_unchanged:\n349\t            if path is None or self.scope.resolve(path, \"read\") is None:\n350\t                return False, f\"{path}: left scope — not restored\"\n351\t            cur = Path(path)\n352\t            cur_hash = _hash_bytes(cur.read_bytes()) if cur.exists() else None\n353\t            if inv.get(\"post_hash\") is not None and cur_hash != inv.get(\"post_hash\"):\n354\t                return False, f\"{path}: changed since execute — NOT clobbered\"\n355\t            if inv.get(\"op\") == \"delete-created\" and cur_hash != inv.get(\"post_hash\"):\n356\t                return False, f\"{path}: changed since execute — not deleted\"\n357\t        try:\n358\t            if inv[\"op\"] == \"restore\":\n359\t                shutil.copyfile(inv[\"pre\"], path)\n360\t                if inv.get(\"pre_mode\") is not None:\n361\t                    os.chmod(path, inv[\"pre_mode\"])                 # BLOCK-5: never call a failed restore clean\n381\t        parts = []\n382\t        if failed:\n383\t            parts.append(\"PARTIAL — not restored: \" + \"; \".join(failed))\n384\t        if irreversible:\n385\t            parts.append(f\"step(s) {irreversible} IRREVERSIBLE (shell already ran)\")\n386\t        return n, (\"; \".join(parts) if parts else \"clean rollback\")\n387\t\n388\t    def undo(self, execute_seq: int) -> AgentResult:\n389\t        rec = self.store.get(execute_seq)\n390\t        res = AgentResult(agent=self.name)\n391\t        if rec is None or rec.payload.get(\"signal\") != \"operator.execute\" or rec.payload.get(\"status\") != \"APPLIED\":\n392\t            res.notes.append(f\"seq {execute_seq} is not an applied operator execution\")\n393\t            return res\n394\t        # Scans perception `event` records for the most recent whose authoritative OCR\n3\t`captured_text` contains the subject (all its salient tokens), and serves the VERBATIM OCR span +\n4\tframe ref + timestamp — the owner's own on-screen text, never a paraphrase and never an advisory VLM\n5\tlead. A0 (observe). Reuses the `consolidate.gate` grounding tokenizer + verbatim-span extractor so\n6\trecall grounds exactly like the memory gate.\"\"\"\n7\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n8\t\n9\tfrom typing import Optional\n10\t\n11\tfrom ..consolidate.gate import salient\n12\tfrom ..spine.store import SpineStore\n13\t\n14\tPERCEPTION_SIGNAL = \"perception\"\n15\t\n16\t\n17\tdef _grounded_line(subject_tokens: set[str], captured: str) -> Optional[str]:\n18\t    \"\"\"The verbatim OCR LINE that contains ALL the subject's salient tokens (so the served sighting\n19\t    always actually shows the subject), or None if no single line holds them (red-pen BLOCK-4 — a\n20\t    whole-frame token match whose tokens are scattered across lines is NOT a grounded sighting).\"\"\"\n21\t    for line in captured.splitlines():\n22\t        if line.strip() and subject_tokens <= salient(line):\n23\t            return line.strip()\n24\t    return None\n25\t\n26\t\n27\tdef recall(store: SpineStore, subject: str) -> Optional[dict]:\n28\t    \"\"\"The most recent grounded sighting of `subject`, or None. Grounded = every salient token of the\n29\t    subject appears in that frame's captured OCR text (the authoritative ground truth).\"\"\"\n30\t    subj = salient(subject)\n31\t    if not subj:\n32\t        return None\n33\t    latest = None\n34\t    latest_line = None\n35\t    for r in store.iter_records():\n36\t        if r.kind != \"event\" or r.payload.get(\"signal\") != PERCEPTION_SIGNAL:\n37\t            continue\n38\t        line = _grounded_line(subj, r.payload.get(\"captured_text\") or \"\")\n39\t        if line is not None:                  # subject co-located on a real OCR line → a grounded sighting\n40\t            latest, latest_line = r, line     # seq-ascending iteration → last match is most recent\n41\t    if latest is None:\n42\t        return None\n43\t    return {\n44\t        \"seq\": latest.seq, \"entry_hash\": latest.entry_hash, \"when\": latest.ts,\n45\t        \"frame_sha256\": latest.payload.get(\"frame_sha256\"),\n46\t        \"quote\": latest_line,                 # verbatim OCR line that actually contains the subject\n47\t    }\n48\t"}]},"uuid":"c59e9007-dd90-49d4-a092-01226ff8f53b","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:56:48.049Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"96e1d687-b6b3-4cdc-b587-d0c02e4605fd","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"9fa3b973-a721-44a5-ad20-bf0d617654a8","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"0268ad75-d9be-4c45-a80f-a1298654d281","agentId":"a1dbf50fd7cad1ed9","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01KtLVH3X8Vs2cgUSk2fb2tu","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Persistent low-latency camera stream (Phase 8, WS-F F2) — a `voice.backends.MicAudioSource`-style\n2\tgenerator over ONE warm `ffmpeg` rawvideo pipe. Unlike `capture.grab_camera` (which cold-starts an\n3\tffmpeg process + writes a PNG + OCRs PER FRAME — hundreds of ms, unusable for control), this keeps the\n4\tdevice handle open and reads raw RGB frames off stdout. Drop-to-latest so inference never queues\n5\tbehind a slow frame. Honest-empty (no frames) if ffmpeg/device/numpy absent — never a fabricated\n6\tframe. `grab_camera` is left untouched (backward-compatible single-shot).\"\"\"\n7\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n8\t\n9\timport shutil\n10\timport subprocess\n11\tfrom pathlib import Path\n12\tfrom typing import Iterator, List, Optional\n13\t\n14\t\n15\tclass CameraStreamSource:\n16\t    def __init__(self, device: str = \"/dev/video0\", width: int = 640, height: int = 480, fps: int = 30):\n17\t        self.device, self.width, self.height, self.fps = device, width, height, fps\n18\t\n19\t    def available(self) -> bool:\n20\t        return bool(shutil.which(\"ffmpeg\")) and Path(self.device).exists()\n21\t\n22\t    def frames(self) -> Iterator:\n23\t        \"\"\"Yield raw RGB frames (numpy HxWx3 uint8) from a single warm ffmpeg process. Ends silently\n24\t        if unavailable.\"\"\"\n25\t        if not self.available():\n26\t            return\n27\t        try:\n28\t            import numpy as np\n29\t        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 — no numpy → no stream (honest)\n30\t            return\n31\t        frame_bytes = self.width * self.height * 3\n32\t        proc: Optional[subprocess.Popen] = None\n33\t        try:\n34\t            proc = subprocess.Popen(\n35\t                [\"ffmpeg\", \"-loglevel\", \"quiet\", \"-f\", \"v4l2\", \"-i\", self.device,\n36\t                 \"-f\", \"rawvideo\", \"-pix_fmt\", \"rgb24\", \"-s\", f\"{self.width}x{self.height}\",\n37\t                 \"-r\", str(self.fps), \"pipe:1\"],\n38\t                stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL)\n39\t            while True:\n40\t                buf = proc.stdout.read(frame_bytes)\n41\t                if not buf or len(buf) < frame_bytes:\n42\t                    break\n43\t                yield np.frombuffer(buf, dtype=np.uint8).reshape((self.height, self.width, 3))\n44\t        except (OSError, ValueError):\n45\t            return\n46\t        finally:\n47\t            if proc is not None:\n48\t                proc.terminate()\n49\t                try:\n50\t                    proc.wait(timeout=2)\n51\t                except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001\n52\t                    proc.kill()\n53\t\n54\t\n55\tclass ScriptedFrameSource:\n56\t    \"\"\"Deterministic double — yields a fixed list of frames (ndarrays or any placeholder). No device.\"\"\"\n57\t    def __init__(self, frames: List):\n58\t        self._frames = list(frames)\n59\t\n60\t    def available(self) -> bool:\n61\t        return True\n62\t\n63\t    def frames(self) -> Iterator:\n64\t        yield from self._frames\n65\t"}]},"uuid":"59b1d2d1-cf2e-483f-9898-2f4960e1475a","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:56:48.562Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"9fa3b973-a721-44a5-ad20-bf0d617654a8","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"cecca256-22b5-4dd9-bddb-bfa3f724ec11","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"0268ad75-d9be-4c45-a80f-a1298654d281","agentId":"a1dbf50fd7cad1ed9","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_012wUmE8ZQGbq27LqBGSdNtr","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"The VLM seam (SIGIL §8 / C4-C6). A `VisionModel` turns a captured Frame + a question into a\n2\tfree-text visual reading. That reading is ADVISORY — the perception layer serves the frame's\n3\tcaptured OCR TEXT as the authoritative answer and labels the model's reading as unverified (see\n4\t`perceive.compose_perception`). Cascade doctrine: a cheap LOCAL model runs freely (on-box, A0); the\n5\tFRONTIER model is a data-egress hop and is gated (see `perception.egress`).\n6\t\n7\tProviders:\n8\t  • `MoondreamVision` — a local moondream-class VLM via Ollama. Fully offline, zero egress → A0.\n9\t    The default reader. Returns '' on any failure (honest empty, never a fabricated reading).\n10\t  • `ClaudeVision` — the frontier Anthropic vision API. `describe()` performs a REAL image UPLOAD\n11\t    (private screen bytes leave the box), so it MUST only be invoked through `perception.egress`,\n12\t    which classifies the upload A2 via the WARDEN oracle and requires a verified owner approval.\n13\t    Never call `ClaudeVision.describe` on an auto path.\"\"\"\n14\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n15\t\n16\timport base64\n17\timport json\n18\timport os\n19\timport urllib.error\n20\timport urllib.request\n21\tfrom pathlib import Path\n22\tfrom typing import Optional, Protocol, runtime_checkable\n23\t\n24\tfrom .capture import Frame\n25\t\n26\t\n27\t@runtime_checkable\n28\tclass VisionModel(Protocol):\n29\t    # `egresses` = does describe() send bytes off the box? On-box models (moondream) are False and may\n30\t    # run on the auto A0 path; frontier models are True and may ONLY be invoked through the egress gate\n31\t    # (`Perceptor.frontier`). `perceive`/`ambient_watch` STRUCTURALLY refuse an egressing model.\n32\t    egresses: bool\n33\t    def describe(self, frame: Frame, question: str) -> str: ...   # advisory free-text reading\n34\t\n35\t\n36\tdef _b64_image(frame: Frame) -> Optional[str]:\n37\t    if not frame.image_path:\n38\t        return None\n39\t    try:\n40\t        return base64.b64encode(Path(frame.image_path).read_bytes()).decode(\"ascii\")\n41\t    except OSError:\n42\t        return None\n43\t\n44\t\n45\tclass MoondreamVision:\n46\t    \"\"\"Local VLM (Ollama, on-box, A0 — nothing leaves the machine). POSTs the frame image + question\n47\t    to `{host}/api/generate` with `images:[b64]`. Mirrors `consolidate.extract.LocalProvider`.\"\"\"\n48\t    egresses = False   # on-box — safe on the auto perceive/ambient path\n49\t\n50\t    def __init__(self, model: str = \"moondream\", host: Optional[str] = None, timeout: int = 60):\n51\t        self.model = model\n52\t        self.host = host or os.environ.get(\"OLLAMA_HOST\") or \"http://127.0.0.1:11434\"\n53\t        self.timeout = timeout\n54\t\n55\t    def describe(self, frame: Frame, question: str) -> str:\n56\t        b64 = _b64_image(frame)\n57\t        if b64 is None:\n58\t            return \"\"\n59\t        body = json.dumps({\"model\": self.model, \"prompt\": (question or \"Describe what is visible.\"),\n60\t                           \"images\": [b64], \"stream\": False}).encode(\"utf-8\")\n61\t        req = urllib.request.Request(f\"{self.host.rstrip('/')}/api/generate\", data=body, method=\"POST\",\n62\t                                     headers={\"content-type\": \"application/json\"})\n63\t        try:\n64\t            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=self.timeout) as resp:\n65\t                payload = json.loads(resp.read().decode(\"utf-8\"))\n66\t        except (urllib.error.URLError, OSError, ValueError):\n67\t            return \"\"   # no reading (honest empty), never a fabricated one\n68\t        return str(payload.get(\"response\", \"\")).strip()[:2000]\n69\t\n70\t\n71\tclass ClaudeVision:\n72\t    \"\"\"FRONTIER Anthropic vision API — `describe()` UPLOADS the image (data egress). Gate it via\n73\t    `perception.egress`; do not call directly on an auto path. Mirrors `extract.ApiProvider`.\"\"\"\n74\t    egresses = True    # sends bytes off-box — perceive/ambient refuse it; only frontier() may use it\n75\t\n76\t    def __init__(self, model: str = \"claude-sonnet-5\", api_key: Optional[str] = None,\n77\t                 timeout: int = 60, max_tokens: int = 512):\n78\t        self.model = model\n79\t        self.api_key = api_key or os.environ.get(\"SIGIL_ANTHROPIC_API_KEY\") or os.environ.get(\"ANTHROPIC_API_KEY\")\n80\t        self.timeout = timeout\n81\t        self.max_tokens = max_tokens\n82\t\n83\t    def describe(self, frame: Frame, question: str) -> str:\n84\t        b64 = _b64_image(frame)\n85\t        if b64 is None or not self.api_key:\n86\t            return \"\"\n87\t        media = \"image/png\" if (frame.image_path or \"\").lower().endswith(\".png\") else \"image/jpeg\"\n88\t        body = json.dumps({\n89\t            \"model\": self.model, \"max_tokens\": self.max_tokens,\n90\t            \"messages\": [{\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": [\n91\t                {\"type\": \"image\", \"source\": {\"type\": \"base64\", \"media_type\": media, \"data\": b64}},\n92\t                {\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": (question or \"Describe what is visible.\")},\n93\t            ]}],\n94\t        }).encode(\"utf-8\")\n95\t        req = urllib.request.Request(\n96\t            \"https://api.anthropic.com/v1/messages\", data=body, method=\"POST\",\n97\t            headers={\"content-type\": \"application/json\", \"x-api-key\": self.api_key,\n98\t                     \"anthropic-version\": \"2023-06-01\"})\n99\t        try:\n100\t            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=self.timeout) as resp:\n101\t                payload = json.loads(resp.read().decode(\"utf-8\"))\n102\t        except (urllib.error.URLError, OSError, ValueError):\n103\t            return \"\"\n104\t        return \"\".join(b.get(\"text\", \"\") for b in payload.get(\"content\", [])\n105\t                       if isinstance(b, dict)).strip()[:2000]\n106\t"}]},"uuid":"1c729dbf-ca98-421d-a857-1f8ef4e1d6e4","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:56:48.570Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"cecca256-22b5-4dd9-bddb-bfa3f724ec11","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"5b92e61e-18e4-4aee-93ed-e2ac331a8f93","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"0268ad75-d9be-4c45-a80f-a1298654d281","agentId":"a1dbf50fd7cad1ed9","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_019scjXBDkSzgBnjQwVwtuWD","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"BridgeDaemon (Phase 7, WS-D D-v) — the desktop-side API the phone talks to over WireGuard. It\n2\treuses the owner-signed cores: the queue (`approvals.pending` with the authorized-device set), a\n3\tDEVICE-signed approval (accepted only while that device is authorized), a PANIC halt (any engage is\n4\tfail-safe), and a KERNEL command relay (`KernelDispatch` → the same WARDEN gate as voice/UI).\n5\t\n6\tThis module is the bridge LIBRARY CORE + API (the doctrine + crypto). The network TRANSPORT — an\n7\tHTTP server over WireGuard, reusing the WS-C two-plane server pattern bound to a `bind_ok` address —\n8\tis a documented NEXT slice, not shipped here. `bind_ok` is the bind predicate that transport MUST\n9\tuse: loopback or a PRIVATE (WireGuard) address only — NEVER 0.0.0.0 / a public address.\"\"\"\n10\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n11\t\n12\timport ipaddress\n13\tfrom typing import List, Optional\n14\t\n15\tfrom ..spine.store import SpineStore\n16\t\n17\t\n18\tdef bind_ok(addr: str) -> bool:\n19\t    \"\"\"True iff `addr` is safe to bind: loopback or a PRIVATE (e.g. WireGuard) address — never\n20\t    0.0.0.0 / a public / an unspecified address.\"\"\"\n21\t    try:\n22\t        ip = ipaddress.ip_address(addr)\n23\t    except ValueError:\n24\t        return False\n25\t    if ip.is_unspecified:                          # 0.0.0.0 / :: → refuse\n26\t        return False\n27\t    return ip.is_loopback or ip.is_private\n28\t\n29\t\n30\tclass BridgeDaemon:\n31\t    def __init__(self, store: Optional[SpineStore] = None, *, trusted_pubkey: Optional[str] = None):\n32\t        from ..governor.identity import owner_pubkey\n33\t        self.store = store or SpineStore()\n34\t        # the owner trust anchor — the persisted identity by default (injectable for tests)\n35\t        self.trusted_pubkey = trusted_pubkey if trusted_pubkey is not None else owner_pubkey()\n36\t\n37\t    def _authorized(self):\n38\t        from ..mesh import authorized_devices\n39\t        return authorized_devices(self.store, self.trusted_pubkey)\n40\t\n41\t    def pending(self) -> List[dict]:\n42\t        \"\"\"The phone-facing queue — minimal fields only (no subject leaks over the tunnel).\"\"\"\n43\t        from ..agents.approvals import pending\n44\t        items = pending(self.store, self.trusted_pubkey, extra_pubkeys=self._authorized())\n45\t        return [{\"seq\": r.seq, \"tier\": r.payload.get(\"tier\"), \"kind\": r.kind} for r in items]\n46\t\n47\t    def submit_device_approval(self, payload: dict) -> int:\n48\t        \"\"\"Append a DEVICE-signed approval the phone produced — ONLY if the signing device is\n49\t        currently authorized AND the signature verifies (fail-closed). A rogue/revoked device or a\n50\t        forged signature is refused; the signed target_seq binds (no replay).\"\"\"\n51\t        from ..agents.approvals import SIGNAL as APPROVAL_SIGNAL\n52\t        from ..agents.approvals import verify_approval\n53\t\n54\t        class _Rec:\n55\t            def __init__(self, p): self.payload = p\n56\t        authorized = self._authorized()\n57\t        if payload.get(\"signal\") != APPROVAL_SIGNAL:\n58\t            raise ValueError(\"not an approval record\")\n59\t        if payload.get(\"pubkey\") not in authorized:\n60\t            raise ValueError(\"signing device is not authorized\")\n61\t        if not verify_approval(_Rec(payload), self.trusted_pubkey, extra_pubkeys=authorized):\n62\t            raise ValueError(\"approval signature invalid\")\n63\t        return self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"mesh\", actor=\"DEVICE\",\n64\t                                 payload={**payload, \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"},\n65\t                                 supersedes_id=payload.get(\"target_seq\"))\n66\t\n67\t    def panic_engage(self, *, by: str = \"phone\") -> int:\n68\t        \"\"\"Halt the mesh from the phone. ANY engage halts (fail-safe) — no signature needed for the\n69\t        SAFE direction. Release stays owner-only at the desktop (the dangerous direction is signed).\"\"\"\n70\t        from ..governor.killswitch import SIGNAL as KS_SIGNAL\n71\t        return self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"mesh\", actor=\"DEVICE\",\n72\t                                 payload={\"signal\": KS_SIGNAL, \"state\": \"engaged\", \"by\": by,\n73\t                                          \"reason\": \"panic halt from phone\", \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"})\n74\t\n75\t    def relay(self, text: str) -> str:\n76\t        \"\"\"Relay a KERNEL command — the same T0-router + WARDEN gate + signed action log as voice/UI.\"\"\"\n77\t        from ..voice.dispatch import KernelDispatch\n78\t        return KernelDispatch().send(text)\n79\t"}]},"uuid":"eaea5da4-78a8-47ac-864d-3b9f18bfa9c3","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:57:11.862Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"5b92e61e-18e4-4aee-93ed-e2ac331a8f93","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

Push carries only {seq,tier,kind}\n4\tover the tunnel — no subject, no secret, no cloud. Offense-free.\"\"\"\n5\tfrom ..reuse import assert_no_offense\n6\t\n7\tassert_no_offense()\n8\t\n9\tfrom .daemon import BridgeDaemon, bind_ok  # noqa: E402\n10\tfrom .notifier import PushNotifier  # noqa: E402\n11\t\n12\t__all__ = [\"BridgeDaemon\", \"PushNotifier\", \"bind_ok\"]\n13\t"}]},"uuid":"6c95e675-97d6-4c2d-89ef-1508b83536a4","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:57:12.211Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"afae6c68-c7b0-490d-b99f-524f76026956","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"d1e9bfc0-39a9-49e9-b9d3-c3c8411f5cb7","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"0268ad75-d9be-4c45-a80f-a1298654d281","agentId":"a1dbf50fd7cad1ed9","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01QSg6igdF25vCvmgfJdPGmG","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"PushNotifier (Phase 7, WS-D D-vi) — pushes to the phone when an A2/A3 item QUEUES, fed by the\n2\tSHARED P0 `SpineTailer` (the same primitive the UI SSE feed uses). The push carries ONLY\n3\t`{seq, tier, kind}` — never the subject, never the payload, never a secret — so nothing sensitive\n4\tcrosses even the WireGuard tunnel; the phone fetches any detail on demand over the daemon API. No\n5\tcloud push (that would egress the private subject off owned hardware).\"\"\"\n6\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n7\t\n8\tfrom typing import List, Optional\n9\t\n10\tfrom ..spine.store import SpineStore\n11\tfrom ..spine.tail import SpineTailer\n12\t\n13\t\n14\tclass PushNotifier:\n15\t    def __init__(self, store: Optional[SpineStore] = None, *, since_seq: Optional[int] = None):\n16\t        self.store = store or SpineStore()\n17\t        start = since_seq if since_seq is not None else self.store.next_seq - 1\n18\t        self.tailer = SpineTailer(self.store, since_seq=start)\n19\t\n20\t    def poll(self) -> List[dict]:\n21\t        \"\"\"New pushes since the last poll: one per newly-QUEUED A2/A3 item, minimal fields only.\"\"\"\n22\t        out: List[dict] = []\n23\t        for ev in self.tailer.poll():\n24\t            rec = self.store.get(ev[\"seq\"])\n25\t            p = rec.payload if rec else {}\n26\t            if (p.get(\"decision\") == \"queued\" and p.get(\"status\") == \"awaiting-approval\"\n27\t                    and p.get(\"tier\") in (\"A2\", \"A3\")):\n28\t                out.append({\"seq\": ev[\"seq\"], \"tier\": p.get(\"tier\"), \"kind\": ev[\"kind\"]})   # no subject/secret\n29\t        return out\n30\t"}]},"uuid":"248a72e5-e8ef-4d5d-9f73-133559ef8487","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:57:12.711Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"d1e9bfc0-39a9-49e9-b9d3-c3c8411f5cb7","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"62fdc59e-3c0a-4508-88df-7d8f6bcd5035","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"0268ad75-d9be-4c45-a80f-a1298654d281","agentId":"a1dbf50fd7cad1ed9","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01KZKzjpJstyS9VYzx4swyDN","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"The mesh registry (Phase 7, WS-D). Two owner-signed ledgers on the one spine:\n2\t\n3\t  • host_capability — each host advertises {os, screen, camera, gpu_vlm, always_on}, owner-signed, so\n4\t    an agent can route work to the best-capable ONLINE host from cited, tamper-evident records.\n5\t  • device authorization — a 1-of-N device ledger: the owner AUTHORIZES a phone's own Ed25519 device\n6\t    key with a signed `device_authorized` record (and `device_revoked` to remove it). The phone then\n7\t    approves A2/A3 items by signing with ITS key; `verify_approval(..., extra_pubkeys=authorized)`\n8\t    accepts it. Trust is pinned to owner-MINTED keys (RP-APPROVAL-2); the phone never holds the\n9\t    trust-root key, and can approve offline.\n10\t\n11\tAll reads are fail-closed: a descriptor/authorization that does not verify against the owner key is\n12\tignored. Reuses `governor.authn` (the same signed-event primitive kill/promotion/approval use).\"\"\"\n13\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n14\t\n15\tfrom typing import List, Optional, Set\n16\t\n17\tfrom ..agents.approvals import SIGNAL as _APPROVAL_SIGNAL\n18\tfrom ..agents.approvals import _approval_message\n19\tfrom ..governor.authn import signed_payload, verify_signed\n20\tfrom ..governor.identity import owner_pubkey\n21\tfrom ..reuse import sha256_hex, sign\n22\tfrom ..spine.store import SpineStore\n23\t\n24\tCAP_SIGNAL = \"mesh.host_capability\"\n25\tDEV_SIGNAL = \"mesh.device\"\n26\t_CAP_CORE = (\"signal\", \"host_id\", \"os\", \"has_screen\", \"has_camera\", \"has_gpu_vlm\", \"always_on\",\n27\t             \"has_hid_inject\", \"has_camera_stream\")\n28\t_DEV_CORE = (\"signal\", \"state\", \"device_id\", \"device_pubkey\")\n29\t\n30\t\n31\t# --- host capability advertisement -----------------------------------------------------------------\n32\tdef advertise_capability(store: SpineStore, descriptor: dict, owner_key) -> int:\n33\t    core = {\"signal\": CAP_SIGNAL, \"host_id\": descriptor[\"host_id\"], \"os\": descriptor[\"os\"],\n34\t            \"has_screen\": bool(descriptor[\"has_screen\"]), \"has_camera\": bool(descriptor[\"has_camera\"]),\n35\t            \"has_gpu_vlm\": bool(descriptor[\"has_gpu_vlm\"]), \"always_on\": bool(descriptor[\"always_on\"]),\n36\t            \"has_hid_inject\": bool(descriptor.get(\"has_hid_inject\", False)),\n37\t            \"has_camera_stream\": bool(descriptor.get(\"has_camera_stream\", False))}\n38\t    payload = {**signed_payload(core, owner_key), \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n39\t    return store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"mesh\", actor=\"OWNER\", payload=payload)\n40\t\n41\t\n42\tdef capability_map(store: SpineStore, trusted_pubkey: Optional[str] = None) -> dict:\n43\t    \"\"\"Latest VERIFIED capability per host_id (forged/unsigned advertisements ignored).\"\"\"\n44\t    tp = trusted_pubkey if trusted_pubkey is not None else owner_pubkey()\n45\t    latest: dict = {}\n46\t    for r in store.iter_records():\n47\t        p = r.payload\n48\t        if p.get(\"signal\") == CAP_SIGNAL and verify_signed(p, _CAP_CORE, tp):\n49\t            latest[p.get(\"host_id\")] = {k: p.get(k) for k in _CAP_CORE if k != \"signal\"}\n50\t    return latest\n51\t\n52\t\n53\t# --- device authorization ledger -------------------------------------------------------------------\n54\tdef authorize_device(store: SpineStore, device_id: str, device_pubkey: str, owner_key) -> int:\n55\t    core = {\"signal\": DEV_SIGNAL, \"state\": \"authorized\", \"device_id\": device_id, \"device_pubkey\": device_pubkey}\n56\t    payload = {**signed_payload(core, owner_key), \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n57\t    return store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"mesh\", actor=\"OWNER\", payload=payload)\n58\t\n59\t\n60\tdef revoke_device(store: SpineStore, device_id: str, device_pubkey: str, owner_key) -> int:\n61\t    core = {\"signal\": DEV_SIGNAL, \"state\": \"revoked\", \"device_id\": device_id, \"device_pubkey\": device_pubkey}\n62\t    payload = {**signed_payload(core, owner_key), \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n63\t    return store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"mesh\", actor=\"OWNER\", payload=payload)\n64\t\n65\t\n66\tdef authorized_devices(store: SpineStore, trusted_pubkey: Optional[str] = None) -> Set[str]:\n67\t    \"\"\"The set of currently-authorized device pubkeys (owner-signed authorize, minus later revoke).\n68\t    An unsigned/forged authorization is ignored — a rogue device cannot self-authorize.\"\"\"\n69\t    tp = trusted_pubkey if trusted_pubkey is not None else owner_pubkey()\n70\t    state: dict = {}\n71\t    for r in store.iter_records():\n72\t        p = r.payload\n73\t        if p.get(\"signal\") == DEV_SIGNAL and p.get(\"state\") in (\"authorized\", \"revoked\") \\\n74\t                and verify_signed(p, _DEV_CORE, tp):\n75\t            state[p.get(\"device_pubkey\")] = p[\"state\"]      # latest verified state per device key\n76\t    return {pub for pub, st in state.items() if st == \"authorized\"}\n77\t\n78\t\n79\tclass DeviceApprover:\n80\t    \"\"\"Signs an approval with a DEVICE key (the phone). The record verifies via\n81\t    `verify_approval(record, owner_pubkey, extra_pubkeys=authorized_devices(store))` — i.e. only while\n82\t    the device remains authorized. Never touches the owner trust-root key.\"\"\"\n83\t    def __init__(self, store: SpineStore, *, device_key):\n84\t        self.store = store\n85\t        self.device_key = device_key\n86\t\n87\t    def _decide(self, seq: int, decision: str, approver: str, reason: str) -> int:\n88\t        msg = _approval_message(seq, decision, approver)\n89\t        sig = sign(self.device_key.private_key_b64, msg)\n90\t        payload = {\"signal\": _APPROVAL_SIGNAL, \"approval\": decision, \"target_seq\": seq,\n91\t                   \"approver\": approver, \"reason\": reason, \"pubkey\": self.device_key.public_key_b64,\n92\t                   \"sig\": sig, \"msg_digest\": sha256_hex(msg), \"device\": True, \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n93\t        return self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"mesh\", actor=\"DEVICE\", payload=payload, supersedes_id=seq)\n94\t\n95\t    def approve(self, seq: int, *, approver: str = \"device\", reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n96\t        return self._decide(seq, \"approved\", approver, reason)\n97\t\n98\t    def deny(self, seq: int, *, approver: str = \"device\", reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n99\t        return self._decide(seq, \"denied\", approver, reason)\n100\t"}]},"uuid":"3358b3bd-46d4-4a0c-b1a4-23ec66dedaf2","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:57:13.663Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"62fdc59e-3c0a-4508-88df-7d8f6bcd5035","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"fd130255-63b9-4804-b332-2d10e0761de1","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"0268ad75-d9be-4c45-a80f-a1298654d281","agentId":"a1dbf50fd7cad1ed9","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01TnDmokCyh9rFHMXTj4nCyH","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Action gate (Phase 8, WS-G G-iii) — a near-verbatim mirror of `perception/egress.py`, but for web\n2\tACTIONS. `action_token = sha256(service|step|url|page_sha256|field_binding)` binds an approval to ONE\n3\texact action — the destination URL is IN the preimage (so an approval can never be rebound to a\n4\tdifferent URL, even one serving identical bytes), alongside the page hash (a page-change aborts) and\n5\tthe field binding (a vault-version bump / literal edit aborts). `action_approved` requires a VERIFIED\n6\towner/authorized-device approval whose signed `target_seq` IS that step — a replay onto a different\n7\taction, a wrong token, an unsigned/denied approval → False (fail-closed).\"\"\"\n8\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n9\t\n10\tfrom ..reuse import sha256_hex\n11\tfrom .approvals import SIGNAL as _APPROVAL_SIGNAL\n12\tfrom .approvals import verify_approval\n13\t\n14\tACTION_SIGNAL = \"web.actor.step\"\n15\t\n16\t\n17\tdef action_token(service: str, step_kind: str, url: str, page_sha256: str, field_binding: str) -> str:\n18\t    return sha256_hex(f\"{service}|{step_kind}|{url}|{page_sha256}|{field_binding}\".encode(\"utf-8\"))\n19\t\n20\t\n21\tdef action_approved(store, seq: int, token: str, trusted_pubkey) -> bool:\n22\t    rec = store.get(seq)\n23\t    if rec is None or rec.payload.get(\"signal\") != ACTION_SIGNAL or rec.payload.get(\"action_token\") != token:\n24\t        return False\n25\t    from ..mesh import authorized_devices\n26\t    devices = authorized_devices(store, trusted_pubkey)\n27\t    for r in store.iter_records(since_seq=seq):\n28\t        p = r.payload\n29\t        if (p.get(\"signal\") == _APPROVAL_SIGNAL and p.get(\"target_seq\") == seq\n30\t                and p.get(\"approval\") == \"approved\" and verify_approval(r, trusted_pubkey, extra_pubkeys=devices)):\n31\t            return True\n32\t    return False\n33\t"}]},"uuid":"475392c6-64a1-45c4-b798-86fe18f482cb","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:57:21.916Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"fd130255-63b9-4804-b332-2d10e0761de1","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"475392c6-64a1-45c4-b798-86fe18f482cb","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a1dbf50fd7cad1ed9","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdAMEpTRxpd2CAEZMrbDB","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_018caYvyFYAv9sSB6kX4Jr3q","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/home/kali/sigil/sigil/perception/capture.py"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":6322,"cache_read_input_tokens":58776,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":6322,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdAMEoT9h8k5LGFUPqyb4","attributionAgent":"Explore","type":"assistant","uuid":"ba8df903-8a0d-475a-9ce2-56623d25f91c","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:57:22.721Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"ba8df903-8a0d-475a-9ce2-56623d25f91c","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"0268ad75-d9be-4c45-a80f-a1298654d281","agentId":"a1dbf50fd7cad1ed9","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_018caYvyFYAv9sSB6kX4Jr3q","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Capture backends (SIGIL §8). A `Frame` is the immutable record of one capture: the image bytes'\n2\tsha256 (integrity), the OCR/accessibility TEXT (the GROUND TRUTH a query is answered from), and a\n3\tpath to the image. Real backends shell out to whatever is installed (screenshot tool + tesseract,\n4\tv4l2/ffmpeg); every backend returns `None` on any failure — a missing camera is no capture, never a\n5\tfabricated one (same posture as SCHOLAR.read_source). `StaticFrame` is the deterministic test double.\n6\t\n7\tHONEST GAPS: headless hosts often lack a screenshot tool / tesseract / a camera, so the real\n8\tbackends degrade to `None` or an image-only Frame (text=\"\"). The richest real screen source is the\n9\tOS accessibility tree (C4); it is not available on a headless box and is left as a documented seam.\"\"\"\n10\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n11\t\n12\timport shutil\n13\timport subprocess\n14\timport tempfile\n15\tfrom dataclasses import dataclass\n16\tfrom pathlib import Path\n17\tfrom typing import Optional\n18\t\n19\tfrom ..reuse import sha256_hex\n20\t\n21\t_SCREENSHOT_TOOLS = (\n22\t    (\"scrot\", [\"scrot\", \"-o\"]),\n23\t    (\"gnome-screenshot\", [\"gnome-screenshot\", \"-f\"]),\n24\t    (\"spectacle\", [\"spectacle\", \"-b\", \"-n\", \"-o\"]),\n25\t    (\"import\", [\"import\", \"-window\", \"root\"]),   # ImageMagick\n26\t)\n27\t\n28\t\n29\t@dataclass(frozen=True)\n30\tclass Frame:\n31\t    kind: str                       # \"screen\" | \"camera\"\n32\t    sha256: str                     # integrity of the captured image bytes\n33\t    text: str                       # OCR / accessibility text — the GROUND TRUTH; \"\" if none\n34\t    image_path: Optional[str] = None\n35\t    width: int = 0\n36\t    height: int = 0\n37\t\n38\t    @classmethod\n39\t    def from_image(cls, kind: str, image_path: str, *, text: str = \"\") -> \"Frame\":\n40\t        data = Path(image_path).read_bytes()\n41\t        return cls(kind=kind, sha256=sha256_hex(data), text=text, image_path=image_path)\n42\t\n43\t\n44\tdef _ocr(image_path: str) -> str:\n45\t    \"\"\"Extract text via tesseract if present; '' otherwise (no OCR is not a failure of capture).\"\"\"\n46\t    if not shutil.which(\"tesseract\"):\n47\t        return \"\"\n48\t    try:\n49\t        proc = subprocess.run([\"tesseract\", image_path, \"stdout\"],\n50\t                              capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=30)\n51\t        return (proc.stdout or \"\").strip()\n52\t    except (subprocess.SubprocessError, OSError):\n53\t        return \"\"\n54\t\n55\t\n56\tdef grab_screen() -> Optional[Frame]:\n57\t    \"\"\"Screenshot the current display + OCR it. Returns None if no screenshot tool is available\n58\t    or the grab fails. The accessibility-tree path (richer, C4) is a documented seam, not wired\n59\t    on headless hosts.\"\"\"\n60\t    out = tempfile.mktemp(suffix=\".png\", prefix=\"sigil-screen-\")\n61\t    for name, argv in _SCREENSHOT_TOOLS:\n62\t        if not shutil.which(name):\n63\t            continue\n64\t        try:\n65\t            proc = subprocess.run([*argv, out], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=20)\n66\t        except (subprocess.SubprocessError, OSError):\n67\t            continue\n68\t        if proc.returncode == 0 and Path(out).exists() and Path(out).stat().st_size > 0:\n69\t            return Frame.from_image(\"screen\", out, text=_ocr(out))\n70\t    return None\n71\t\n72\t\n73\tdef grab_camera(device: str = \"/dev/video0\") -> Optional[Frame]:\n74\t    \"\"\"Grab a single frame from a v4l2 camera via ffmpeg. Returns None if ffmpeg or the device is\n75\t    absent, or the grab fails. Camera frames rarely carry text, so OCR is best-effort.\"\"\"\n76\t    if not shutil.which(\"ffmpeg\") or not Path(device).exists():\n77\t        return None\n78\t    out = tempfile.mktemp(suffix=\".png\", prefix=\"sigil-cam-\")\n79\t    try:\n80\t        proc = subprocess.run(\n81\t            [\"ffmpeg\", \"-y\", \"-f\", \"v4l2\", \"-i\", device, \"-frames:v\", \"1\", out],\n82\t            capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=20)\n83\t    except (subprocess.SubprocessError, OSError):\n84\t        return None\n85\t    if proc.returncode == 0 and Path(out).exists() and Path(out).stat().st_size > 0:\n86\t        return Frame.from_image(\"camera\", out, text=_ocr(out))\n87\t    return None\n88\t\n89\t\n90\tdef StaticFrame(*, kind: str = \"screen\", text: str = \"\", tag: str = \"\") -> Frame:\n91\t    \"\"\"Deterministic test/double frame — no filesystem, sha256 derived from (tag or text) so\n92\t    identical content hashes identically (drives ambient change-detection).\"\"\"\n93\t    return Frame(kind=kind, sha256=sha256_hex((tag or text).encode()), text=text, image_path=None)\n94\t"}]},"uuid":"6e5ec805-ad8c-4af9-b664-cb04fcc49017","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:57:22.757Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"ba8df903-8a0d-475a-9ce2-56623d25f91c","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"6e5ec805-ad8c-4af9-b664-cb04fcc49017","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a1dbf50fd7cad1ed9","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdAMEpTRxpd2CAEZMrbDB","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_0139mJkFxRKUBZpnHyvRW4UL","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/home/kali/sigil/sigil/perception/egress.py"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":6322,"cache_read_input_tokens":58776,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":6322,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdAMEoT9h8k5LGFUPqyb4","attributionAgent":"Explore","type":"assistant","uuid":"c96fe585-5c71-4d5d-bbd8-b473b8fc1bf0","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:57:22.930Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"c96fe585-5c71-4d5d-bbd8-b473b8fc1bf0","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"0268ad75-d9be-4c45-a80f-a1298654d281","agentId":"a1dbf50fd7cad1ed9","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_0139mJkFxRKUBZpnHyvRW4UL","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Frontier-vision EGRESS gate (Phase 7, WS-A V-ii). Sending a screen/camera frame to the frontier\n2\tAnthropic VLM uploads private bytes off the owned machine — that is A2 data-egress, not a free\n3\t\"try local then frontier\" quality bump. This gate makes the cascade doctrine-honest:\n4\t\n5\t  • The tier is DERIVED from the WARDEN oracle (`KernelClassifier` on `vision.frontier.upload` → A2),\n6\t    never self-declared.\n7\t  • Nothing uploads until a VERIFIED owner approval exists that is BOUND to this exact egress — an\n8\t    `egress_token = sha256(frame.sha256 | question)`. An approval of a different egress (a replay)\n9\t    does not match `target_seq` and does not authorize the upload.\n10\t  • Absent approval, the egress is QUEUED (an A2 proposal recorded on the spine) and the frontier\n11\t    reading is WITHHELD; the local (on-box) reading still stands.\n12\t\n13\tReuses the hardened signed `ApprovalQueue` verification (`verify_approval`) — the same fail-closed\n14\towner-key check the mesh already trusts.\"\"\"\n15\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n16\t\n17\tfrom ..agents.approvals import SIGNAL as _APPROVAL_SIGNAL\n18\tfrom ..agents.approvals import verify_approval\n19\tfrom ..reuse import sha256_hex\n20\t\n21\tEGRESS_SIGNAL = \"vision.egress\"\n22\tFRONTIER_TOOL = \"vision.frontier.upload\"   # classified A2 by the WARDEN oracle (contains \"upload\")\n23\t\n24\t\n25\tdef egress_token(frame_sha256: str, question: str) -> str:\n26\t    \"\"\"A stable id binding an approval to exactly this (frame, question) upload.\"\"\"\n27\t    return sha256_hex(f\"{frame_sha256}|{question or ''}\".encode(\"utf-8\"))\n28\t\n29\t\n30\tdef egress_approved(store, seq: int, token: str, trusted_pubkey) -> bool:\n31\t    \"\"\"True iff `seq` is an egress request bound to `token` AND carries a VERIFIED approval by the\n32\t    owner OR an owner-authorized device. Fail-closed: wrong token, no approval, denied, or an\n33\t    approval of a different seq → False.\"\"\"\n34\t    rec = store.get(seq)\n35\t    if rec is None or rec.payload.get(\"signal\") != EGRESS_SIGNAL or rec.payload.get(\"egress_token\") != token:\n36\t        return False\n37\t    from ..mesh import authorized_devices\n38\t    devices = authorized_devices(store, trusted_pubkey)\n39\t    for r in store.iter_records(since_seq=seq):\n40\t        p = r.payload\n41\t        if (p.get(\"signal\") == _APPROVAL_SIGNAL and p.get(\"target_seq\") == seq\n42\t                and p.get(\"approval\") == \"approved\" and verify_approval(r, trusted_pubkey, extra_pubkeys=devices)):\n43\t            return True\n44\t    return False\n45\t"}]},"uuid":"7d249bf9-f3c1-4932-9fcd-499ffd7531a8","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:57:22.970Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"c96fe585-5c71-4d5d-bbd8-b473b8fc1bf0","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"40bc0947-43df-4dcb-8c6c-4f0e77e6602f","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"0268ad75-d9be-4c45-a80f-a1298654d281","agentId":"a1dbf50fd7cad1ed9","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01C7xBsceiEeg9aBDtzkNt9o","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"SIGIL Perception subsystem (Phase 5, SIGIL §8). On-demand screen/camera capture → a grounded\n2\tperception answer. The DISCIPLINE mirrors SCHOLAR's serve-the-quote gate: the CAPTURED TEXT\n3\t(accessibility tree / OCR) is the AUTHORITATIVE ground truth; the VLM's visual reading is\n4\tADVISORY only, never asserted as the screen's content. Ambient vision (C6) is opt-in, indicator-\n5\tlit, and escalates only on a detected change — nothing persists beyond event records.\n6\t\n7\tOffense-free by doctrine: perception observes the owner's OWN screen/camera; it takes no\n8\texternal action (ceiling A0/A1).\"\"\"\n9\tfrom ..reuse import assert_no_offense\n10\t\n11\tassert_no_offense()\n12\t\n13\tfrom .capture import Frame, grab_camera, grab_screen  # noqa: E402\n14\tfrom .delta import changed  # noqa: E402\n15\tfrom .perceive import Perceptor, compose_perception  # noqa: E402\n16\tfrom .recall import recall  # noqa: E402\n17\tfrom .veracity import corroborate  # noqa: E402\n18\tfrom .vision import ClaudeVision, MoondreamVision, VisionModel  # noqa: E402\n19\t\n20\t__all__ = [\"Frame\", \"grab_screen\", \"grab_camera\", \"Perceptor\", \"compose_perception\",\n21\t           \"VisionModel\", \"ClaudeVision\", \"MoondreamVision\", \"corroborate\", \"recall\", \"changed\"]\n22\t"}]},"uuid":"7f162ad2-bef2-4e45-85aa-debb87600b57","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:57:23.261Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"40bc0947-43df-4dcb-8c6c-4f0e77e6602f","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

Graceful fallbacks; components are injectable so the\n4\twhole loop runs deterministically on scripted doubles with no camera/model/OS input.\"\"\"\n5\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n6\t\n7\tfrom typing import Optional\n8\t\n9\tfrom .pipeline import GesturePipeline\n10\tfrom .session import SessionGate\n11\t\n12\t\n13\tdef run_gesture(*, store=None, owner_key=None, source=None, landmarker=None, classifier=None,\n14\t                backend=None, pipeline=None, gate=None, max_frames: Optional[int] = None) -> int:\n15\t    \"\"\"Run the loop; returns the number of frames processed. Injectable for tests.\"\"\"\n16\t    if store is None:\n17\t        from ..spine.store import SpineStore\n18\t        store = SpineStore()\n19\t    if source is None:\n20\t        from ..perception.camera_stream import CameraStreamSource\n21\t        source = CameraStreamSource()\n22\t    if landmarker is None:\n23\t        from .landmark import OnnxHandLandmarker\n24\t        landmarker = OnnxHandLandmarker()\n25\t    if classifier is None:\n26\t        from .features import RuleClassifier\n27\t        classifier = RuleClassifier()\n28\t    if backend is None:\n29\t        from ..platform import input_backend\n30\t        backend = input_backend()\n31\t    pipe = pipeline or GesturePipeline()\n32\t    g = gate or SessionGate(store, backend, owner_key=owner_key)\n33\t    g.arm(owner_key=owner_key)\n34\t    n = 0\n35\t    try:\n36\t        for frame in source.frames():\n37\t            reading = classifier.classify(landmarker.detect(frame))\n38\t            intent = pipe.on_frame(reading)\n39\t            if intent is not None:\n40\t                g.handle(intent)\n41\t            n += 1\n42\t            if max_frames is not None and n >= max_frames:\n43\t                break\n44\t    finally:\n45\t        g.disarm()\n46\t    return n\n47\t"}]},"uuid":"81f9e770-9a4b-41a4-9f2d-29c1450b3b6d","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:57:45.582Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"f3f9f373-d6f0-4cf0-8403-9bb42eadd76c","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"6047db48-9d58-4fa3-b874-6662e93016b4","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"0268ad75-d9be-4c45-a80f-a1298654d281","agentId":"a1dbf50fd7cad1ed9","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01AZWgVcWnt35R6exn1zKK3Y","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Read-only operator dashboard (SIGIL §9.4, C39/C40) — a glanceable status surface over the spine:\n2\tkill-switch state, per-agent activity, the tiered decision breakdown (auto/queued/denied), the\n3\tpending approval queue, today's budget usage, and ingest lag. LOOPBACK, READ-ONLY, ZERO-IMPACT: it\n4\tonly reads the append-only log and writes nothing (same posture as the CRUCIBLE ops console). A\n5\tproper TUI/web view renders this same dict; here it renders to text.\"\"\"\n6\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n7\t\n8\tfrom collections import Counter\n9\tfrom datetime import datetime, timezone\n10\tfrom typing import Optional\n11\t\n12\tfrom .spine.store import SpineStore\n13\t\n14\t\n15\tdef snapshot(store: SpineStore, *, day_iso: Optional[str] = None, lookback: int = 300) -> dict:\n16\t    from .agents.approvals import pending\n17\t    from .governor import KillSwitch\n18\t    from .governor.identity import owner_pubkey\n19\t    head = store.next_seq - 1\n20\t    day = day_iso or datetime.now(timezone.utc).date().isoformat()\n21\t\n22\t    per_agent: Counter = Counter()\n23\t    decisions: Counter = Counter()\n24\t    interrupts_today: Counter = Counter()\n25\t    actions_today: Counter = Counter()\n26\t    last_checkpoint = -1\n27\t    last_consolidation = -1\n28\t    for r in store.iter_records():\n29\t        if r.kind == \"warden_checkpoint\":\n30\t            last_checkpoint = r.seq\n31\t        if r.kind in (\"entity\", \"contradiction\") and r.source == \"agent\":\n32\t            last_consolidation = max(last_consolidation, r.seq)\n33\t        if r.source == \"agent\" and (r.ts or \"\").startswith(day):\n34\t            # count only actions actually TAKEN (auto/queued), mirroring the enforced BudgetLedger —\n35\t            # denials must not inflate the usage panel (red-pen RP-2 honesty).\n36\t            if r.payload.get(\"decision\") in (\"auto\", \"queued\"):\n37\t                actions_today[r.actor] += 1\n38\t                if r.kind == \"event\":\n39\t                    interrupts_today[r.actor] += 1\n40\t        if r.source == \"agent\" and r.seq > head - lookback:\n41\t            per_agent[r.actor] += 1\n42\t            decisions[str(r.payload.get(\"decision\"))] += 1\n43\t\n44\t    pend = pending(store, owner_pubkey())\n45\t    return {\n46\t        \"head_seq\": head,\n47\t        \"kill_switch\": \"ENGAGED\" if KillSwitch(store).is_engaged() else \"released\",\n48\t        \"recent_by_agent\": dict(per_agent.most_common()),\n49\t        \"recent_decisions\": dict(decisions),\n50\t        \"pending_approvals\": [{\"seq\": r.seq, \"tier\": r.payload.get(\"tier\"), \"kind\": r.kind,\n51\t                               \"agent\": r.actor, \"subject\": r.payload.get(\"subject\")} for r in pend],\n52\t        \"budget_today\": {a: {\"actions\": actions_today[a], \"interrupts\": interrupts_today[a]}\n53\t                         for a in sorted(actions_today)},\n54\t        \"ingest_lag\": {\"head_seq\": head, \"last_checkpoint_seq\": last_checkpoint,\n55\t                       \"records_since_checkpoint\": (head - last_checkpoint) if last_checkpoint >= 0 else None,\n56\t                       \"last_consolidation_seq\": last_consolidation},\n57\t        \"day\": day,\n58\t    }\n59\t\n60\t\n61\tdef render_dashboard(d: dict) -> str:\n62\t    lines = [f\"# SIGIL dashboard — {d['day']}  (spine head seq {d['head_seq']})\", \"\"]\n63\t    ks = d[\"kill_switch\"]\n64\t    lines.append(f\"Kill switch: {'🛑 ENGAGED — mesh halted (observe only)' if ks == 'ENGAGED' else '● released (mesh live)'}\")\n65\t    lag = d[\"ingest_lag\"]\n66\t    lines.append(f\"Ingest lag: {lag['records_since_checkpoint']} record(s) since last checkpoint \"\n67\t                 f\"(checkpoint seq {lag['last_checkpoint_seq']}, consolidation seq {lag['last_consolidation_seq']})\")\n68\t    lines.append(\"\")\n69\t    lines.append(f\"## Pending approvals ({len(d['pending_approvals'])})\")\n70\t    if d[\"pending_approvals\"]:\n71\t        for a in d[\"pending_approvals\"][:12]:\n72\t            lines.append(f\"- seq {a['seq']} [{a['tier']}] {a['agent']} · {a['kind']}: {a.get('subject')}\"\n73\t                         f\"   → sigil approve {a['seq']} / sigil deny {a['seq']}\")\n74\t    else:\n75\t        lines.append(\"- none awaiting approval.\")\n76\t    lines.append(\"\")\n77\t    lines.append(\"## Recent activity (last window)\")\n78\t    lines.append(\"  agents: \" + (\", \".join(f\"{a} {n}\" for a, n in d[\"recent_by_agent\"].items()) or \"quiet\"))\n79\t    lines.append(\"  decisions: \" + (\", \".join(f\"{k} {v}\" for k, v in d[\"recent_decisions\"].items()) or \"none\"))\n80\t    if d[\"budget_today\"]:\n81\t        lines.append(\"\")\n82\t        lines.append(\"## Budget usage today\")\n83\t        for a, u in d[\"budget_today\"].items():\n84\t            lines.append(f\"  {a}: {u['actions']} action(s), {u['interrupts']} interrupt(s)\")\n85\t    return \"\\n\".join(lines)\n86\t"}]},"uuid":"5e86cbb4-246b-47d8-90f2-d388439d6002","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:57:45.593Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"6047db48-9d58-4fa3-b874-6662e93016b4","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"1680b74d-c0ef-4840-84eb-66027477dfda","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"0268ad75-d9be-4c45-a80f-a1298654d281","agentId":"a1dbf50fd7cad1ed9","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_016tMVmQ4GvoQY3UnVhxvRKL","type":"tool_result","content":"360\t    print(f\"  host {d.host_id} · {d.os}\")\n361\t    print(f\"    screen={d.has_screen} camera={d.has_camera} gpu_vlm={d.has_gpu_vlm} always_on={d.always_on}\")\n362\t\n363\t\n364\tdef cmd_serve(a) -> None:\n365\t    \"\"\"Start the loopback glass-cockpit UI. Mints a fresh session token (printed here); every\n366\t    request needs it and no web page can read it. Read plane is GET-only; the owner-signed action\n367\t    plane is CSRF/Host/Origin-gated.\"\"\"\n368\t    import secrets\n369\t\n370\t    from .ui.server import serve\n371\t    serve(token=secrets.token_urlsafe(24), port=a.port)\n372\t\n373\t\n374\tdef cmd_verify(a) -> None:\n375\t    ok, msg = SpineStore().verify()\n376\t    print((\"chain OK: \" if ok else \"chain FAIL: \") + msg)\n377\t    hok, hmsg = verify_checkpoint()\n378\t    print((\"head  OK: \" if hok else \"head  --: \") + hmsg)\n379\t    sys.exit(0 if ok else 2)\n380\t\n381\t\n382\tdef cmd_status(a) -> None:\n383\t    store, vi = SpineStore(), VectorIndex()\n384\t    ok, msg = store.verify()\n385\t    hok, hmsg = verify_checkpoint()\n386\t    print(f\"spine:     {store.count()} records | next_seq {store.next_seq}\")\n387\t    print(f\"vectors:   {vi.count()} points | last_indexed_seq {vi.last_indexed_seq()}\")\n388\t    print(f\"chain:     {'OK' if ok else 'FAIL'} — {msg}\")\n389\t    print(f\"head:      {'OK' if hok else '(' + hmsg + ')'}\")\n390\t\n391\t\n392\tdef main(argv=None) -> None:\n393\t    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog=\"sigil\")\n394\t    sub = p.add_subparsers(dest=\"cmd\", required=True)\n395\t    pi = sub.add_parser(\"ingest\")\n396\t    pi.add_argument(\"--reset\", action=\"store_true\", help=\"clear spine+cursor+vectors and rebuild\")\n397\t    pi.add_argument(\"--docs\", action=\"store_true\", help=\"also ingest curated memory/*.md\")\n398\t    pi.add_argument(\"--git\", action=\"store_true\", help=\"also ingest git commit history\")\n399\t    pi.add_argument(\"--git-only\", dest=\"git_only\", action=\"store_true\","}]},"uuid":"be4467a8-f027-4b05-a0cd-5b4ba01b7604","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:57:45.595Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"1680b74d-c0ef-4840-84eb-66027477dfda","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
