{"parentUuid":"8fdbf41e-adc9-493b-ab1d-112ee6555096","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a1dbf50fd7cad1ed9","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdAMKzUpSMcHuFu2zZMko","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"I have mapped the full ground truth across all six requested surfaces plus the cross-cutting `bridge`/`mesh` layer that is the actual (partly-unshipped) phone path. Everything below is read-only findings; I made no changes.\n\n---\n\n# SIGIL remote-triggerable surfaces — ground truth for a phone companion\n\n## Key up-front finding: there are TWO server postures, only ONE is shipped\n\n- `sigil/ui/server.py` — **shipped** HTTP server, **127.0.0.1 loopback only**, token-gated. This is the \"glass cockpit.\"\n- `sigil/bridge/daemon.py` — the **phone-over-WireGuard** API, but it is a **library core only**. Its docstring is explicit: *\"The network TRANSPORT — an HTTP server over WireGuard, reusing the WS-C two-plane server pattern bound to a `bind_ok` address — is a documented NEXT slice, **not shipped here**.\"* There is no bridge subcommand in `cli.py`, no `HTTPServer` for it anywhere. So today the phone has crypto + methods + doctrine, but **no network listener exists**.\n\nConsequently, **no SIGIL surface is reachable over WireGuard today.** The bridge is the intended reuse point; the phone app would supply the missing transport around `BridgeDaemon`.\n\n---\n\n## 1. `sigil/ui/` — the glass cockpit (loopback-only)\n\n**Files:** `/home/kali/sigil/sigil/ui/server.py`, `/home/kali/sigil/sigil/ui/actions.py`, `/home/kali/sigil/sigil/ui/static/{index.html,app.js,style.css}`, `/home/kali/sigil/sigil/ui/__init__.py`. Launched by `cli.cmd_serve` (`/home/kali/sigil/sigil/cli.py:364`) → `serve(token=secrets.token_urlsafe(24), port=8733)`.\n\n### Full endpoint list\n\n**Token-free bootstrap (no secret carried):**\n- `GET /`, `GET /index.html` → `_serve_index` (server.py:147). Serves `index.html` with `__SIGIL_TOKEN__` replaced by the live token, injected as `<body data-token=...>` (unreadable cross-origin).\n- `GET /static/app.js`, `GET /static/style.css` → `_serve_static` (server.py:137), allowlisted by `_STATIC_OK = {\"app.js\",\"style.css\"}`.\n\n**READ plane — GET `/api/*`, requires token only (`_token_ok`):**\n- `GET /api/snapshot` → `dashboard.snapshot(store)` (kill-switch, pending approvals, per-agent activity, budget, ingest lag). server.py:122.\n- `GET /api/record/{seq}` → `_record` (server.py:155): one spine record **re-verified LIVE** via `verify_record(rec)` (prove-don't-guess), returns `integrity_ok`/`integrity_reason`.\n- `GET /api/stream` → `_sse` (server.py:190): SSE live feed.\n- `GET /api/graph` → `graph.health()`; `GET /api/graph/entity?name=` → `graph.entity(name)`.\n- `GET /api/classify?tool=` → `_classify` (server.py:182): `{\"tool\":tool,\"tier\": KernelClassifier().classify(tool).label()}`.\n- `GET /api/ask?q=` → `_ask` (server.py:186): **exception** — it dispatches a KERNEL subprocess, so it carries the **FULL action gate** (`_action_ok()`), not just the token (server.py:119-121).\n\n**ACTION plane — POST, requires token + Origin/Referer + Host (`_action_ok`):**\n- `POST /api/action` → `do_POST` (server.py:215) → `actions.do_action(action, body, store)`. The only mutating endpoint.\n\n### Two-plane security model (all in `server.py`)\n- **127.0.0.1 bind:** `build_server` → `UIServer((\"127.0.0.1\", port), ...)` (server.py:236). Never `0.0.0.0`.\n- **X-SIGIL-Token:** `_token_ok` (server.py:70) — `hmac.compare_digest(tok, self.server.token)`, from header `X-SIGIL-Token` or `?token=` (SSE can't set headers). Token minted per-process, printed to terminal.\n- **Origin/Referer + Host allowlist (`_action_ok`, server.py:75):** `Host` must be in `allowed_hosts = {\"127.0.0.1:{port}\",\"localhost:{port}\"}` (anti DNS-rebinding). `Origin`, if present, must **exact-match** `allowed_origins` (a `startswith` would let `http://127.0.0.1:80.evil.com` slip). `Referer`, if present, must sit under an allowed origin. Then `_token_ok()`.\n- **CSRF-by-proof:** the token is embedded in the page as a `data-` attribute a cross-origin page cannot read (`app.js` reads `document.body.dataset.token`), and a cross-origin page cannot forge `Origin`; Host allowlist closes DNS-rebinding. No secret is in a cookie, so there is nothing to auto-attach.\n- **Hardening:** CSP `default-src 'self'; base-uri 'none'; form-action 'none'; frame-ancestors 'none'`, `X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff`, `Referrer-Policy: no-referrer`, body cap 65536, 30s socket timeout, `log_message` suppressed (never logs the token).\n\n### `/api/action` owner-signed approve/deny — **private key stays server-side**\n`actions.do_action(action, params, *, store)` (`actions.py:16`). `ACTIONS = frozenset({\"approve\",\"deny\",\"kill\",\"release\",\"promote\",\"revoke\"})` — fail-closed. The browser sends only `{action, seq, reason}`; the **server** obtains `owner = ensure_owner_keypair()` (the persisted owner identity, auto-created once, *never supplied by the caller*) and signs. Explicitly (module docstring): *\"The browser never holds key material… The private key never touches the browser.\"*\n- approve/deny → `ApprovalQueue(store, owner_key=owner).approve/deny(seq, reason)`.\n- kill/release → `KillSwitch(store, owner_key=owner).engage/release`.\n- promote/revoke → `PromotionPolicy(store, owner_key=owner).grant/revoke(agent, scope)`.\n\n### SSE `/api/stream`\n`_sse` (server.py:190): `text/event-stream`, cursor from `?since=`, `SpineTailer(store, since_seq=since)`, emits each new record as `data: {json}\\n\\n` + `: hb` heartbeat, 0.25s poll. Token via `?token=`.\n\n### Reachability & what a phone needs\n**Loopback only.** A network client cannot reach it unless the request actually arrives on 127.0.0.1 (e.g., an SSH/WireGuard forward to loopback) **and** presents `Host: 127.0.0.1:8733` (or localhost) plus the printed token. The Origin/Referer checks are browser-oriented; a native phone client that omits `Origin`/`Referer` still passes `_action_ok` **if** it sends an allowed `Host` and the token (both are optional-if-absent in the checks). So the cleanest reuse is: forward a loopback port over the tunnel, hand the phone the token, send `X-SIGIL-Token` + `Host: 127.0.0.1:8733`.\n\n---\n\n## 2. `sigil/voice/dispatch.py` — `KernelDispatch.send()`\n\n`/home/kali/sigil/sigil/voice/dispatch.py`. `class KernelDispatch(kernel_bin=None, timeout=60)`; `_default_bin()` resolves the Rust binary (`SIGIL_KERNEL_BIN` env → `kernel/target/release|debug/sigil-kernel` → PATH).\n\n`send(self, text: str) -> str`: runs `subprocess.run([kernel_bin, \"ask\", text], ...)`, strips KERNEL status-line prefixes (`[t0`, `[warden`, `[blocked`, `[direct`, `[dispatch`), returns the answer body (≤600 chars). A `[BLOCKED` line → `\"that action needs your approval — it wasn't run.\"`. **Returns a string.**\n\n**Auth:** none of its own — it funnels to the Rust KERNEL, which applies the **T0 router + WARDEN gate + signed action log**. Voice, the UI `/api/ask`, and `BridgeDaemon.relay` all share this one path. This is a subprocess call, **not a network surface** — remote reach comes only via a wrapper (UI `/api/ask` or bridge `relay`).\n\n---\n\n## 3. `sigil/agents/actor.py` (DELEGATE) + `sigil/agents/operator.py` — plan→approve→execute\n\n### OPERATOR (`operator.py`)\n- `preview(self, steps: List[Step], *, subject=\"\") -> tuple[PreviewReport, AgentResult]` (operator.py:194). Each `Step(op, path, content, argv, cwd)` tiered via `KernelClassifier` on `fs.read/fs.write/fs.delete/shell.exec.<argv0>`. Executable plan (with content) written to **0700 journal** `~/.sigil/operator/plans/{plan_hash}.json`; the spine record is **redacted (hashes only)**, signal `operator.plan`. Queued A2/A3 → `rep.plan_seq`.\n- `execute(self, plan_seq: int) -> AgentResult` (operator.py:240). Re-previews (anti-TOCTOU: `plan_hash` must still match), re-derives tier, and for A2/A3 requires `self._approved(plan_seq)` (operator.py:227): `authorized_devices(store, tp)` + `verify_approval(r, tp, extra_pubkeys=devices)` — **owner OR authorized-device approval both satisfy it.** Journals pre-images, applies atomically, verifies post-image hash; `undo()` is hash-bound + single-shot.\n\n### DELEGATE (`actor.py`)\n- `preview(self, steps: List[WebStep]) -> tuple` → (queued seqs, AgentResult) (actor.py:129). Dry-run GET + block-detect + `ActorScope` origin/creation-cap checks; executable step journaled 0700 at `~/.sigil/actor/steps/{token}.json`; queues an A3 (`signal=\"web.actor.step\"`) carrying `action_token`.\n- `execute(self, step_seq: int) -> AgentResult` (actor.py:190). Requires `action_approved(store, step_seq, token, self._tp())` (`actor_gate.py:21`) — again accepts owner **or** authorized device via `verify_approval(..., extra_pubkeys=devices)`. Single-shot, re-fetch, re-derive token, last-instant credential resolve from the owner's own vault.\n\n### How a queued step surfaces / the seq the phone signs\n- `approvals.pending(store, trusted_pubkey, *, extra_pubkeys)` (`approvals.py:53`) returns **all** records with `decision==\"queued\"` and `status==\"awaiting-approval\"` not yet resolved by a **verified** approval — **signal-agnostic**, so operator plans, actor steps, and gesture actions all appear together.\n- The phone signs Ed25519 over the canonical `{\"target\":seq,\"decision\":...,\"approver\":...}` message (`_approval_message`, approvals.py:28) via `DeviceApprover._decide` (`mesh/registry.py:87`). **The seq it signs is the queued proposal's own spine seq** (`plan_seq`/`step_seq`). `verify_approval` (approvals.py:33) requires the claimed `pubkey ∈ {owner} ∪ authorized_devices` and the signed `target_seq` binds (no replay onto another item).\n\n**Auth:** owner-signed OR owner-authorized-device-signed. **Reachability:** `preview`/`execute` are **local library calls** — not in the CLI `agents` action choices, not on the UI `/api/action` allowlist. The only (designed) remote touch is **approving** their queued items: via UI `/api/action` (owner-signed, loopback) or `BridgeDaemon.submit_device_approval` (device-signed, WireGuard — transport unshipped). **`execute()` always runs on the PC**; a phone can approve a queued step but cannot trigger preview/execute remotely today. Note also: UI `approve` can approve *any* queued seq (operator/actor/gesture) since `pending()` is signal-agnostic, but nothing on the UI/bridge drives the subsequent `execute()`.\n\n---\n\n## 4. `sigil/gesture/session.py` — owner-armed `SessionGate`\n\n`/home/kali/sigil/sigil/gesture/session.py`.\n- `arm(self, *, owner_key=None, ttl_seconds=1800.0) -> Session` (session.py:65): **requires the owner key in-process** (`owner_keypair()`; raises `RuntimeError` without it — BLOCK-3). Appends a **signed** `gesture.session_armed` record via `signed_payload(core, ok)` (owner Ed25519), TTL-bounded. The live gate is the **in-memory `Session`** held only by the owner's running daemon.\n- `disarm(self)` (session.py:80): appends `gesture.session_disarmed` (unsigned record) and clears the in-memory session. Also triggered on `hand_lost` and TTL expiry inside `handle()`.\n- `handle(intent) -> dict` (session.py:89): injects only inside a live, non-expired session and only for **A1** tools (pointer move/click/scroll/drag). **A2+** (`hid.type`/`hid.combo`/`hid.app.launch`) → **queues** an approval bound to `sha256(session_id|tool|args)` (`signal=\"gesture.action\"`, `status=\"awaiting-approval\"`), injects nothing.\n- Only caller: `gesture/run.py run_gesture` (`session.py` armed on start, disarmed in `finally`) — **local daemon only.**\n\n**Could a phone arm/disarm remotely?** **No — there is no remote arm/disarm path.** Arming is authenticated by **local possession of the owner key**, not by a verifiable remote request; the signed `gesture.session_armed` record is an *audit proof*, not a remotely-submittable authorization, and the enforcing session lives only in the local process memory. There is no signature path a phone could use to arm. The **only** remote-touchable gesture piece is that a queued A2 `gesture.action` surfaces in `pending()` and could be approved by an authorized device (same `DeviceApprover` path). Indirect remote \"stop\" exists via `BridgeDaemon.panic_engage` (kill switch), but not arm/disarm. This is the deliberate keystone: **loopback/local-process only.**\n\n---\n\n## 5. `sigil/perception/` — recall, vision, camera_stream, Frame contract\n\n- **Frame contract** (`capture.py:29`): `@dataclass(frozen=True) Frame(kind: \"screen\"|\"camera\", sha256, text, image_path=None, width=0, height=0)`; `Frame.from_image(kind, image_path, *, text=\"\")`. `text` (OCR/accessibility) is the **authoritative ground truth**; `sha256` is image integrity.\n- **Sources are all LOCAL:** `grab_screen()`, `grab_camera(device=\"/dev/video0\")` → `Optional[Frame]` (None on failure). `camera_stream.CameraStreamSource(device=\"/dev/video0\", w, h, fps).frames()` yields raw numpy RGB frames from a warm local ffmpeg v4l2 pipe. `StaticFrame`/`ScriptedFrameSource` are test doubles.\n- **recall** (`recall.py:27`): `recall(store, subject) -> Optional[dict]` — \"where did I last see X?\"; scans perception `event` records for the most recent whose `captured_text` OCR line contains **all** salient subject tokens; returns `{seq, entry_hash, when, frame_sha256, quote}` (verbatim OCR line). A0, grounded.\n- **vision** (`vision.py`): `MoondreamVision` (local Ollama, `egresses=False`, A0) is the default; `ClaudeVision` (frontier Anthropic, `egresses=True`) uploads bytes and may run **only** through `perception/egress.py` (A2, `egress_token = sha256(frame.sha256|question)`, verified owner/device approval).\n\n**Could a phone camera/mic feed the pipeline, or query \"what did I last see\"?** **No remote path today.**\n- **Ingestion:** every `Frame` source is a **local device** (`/dev/video0`, local screenshot tools, or a saved image path). There is **no endpoint that accepts a Frame/image over the network** — a phone cannot POST camera/mic bytes in. The mesh capability ledger advertises `has_camera`/`has_camera_stream` per host so *compute* can be routed to a camera-equipped host, but there is no inbound-frame contract.\n- **Query:** `recall()` is a library function with **no network endpoint** — it is not in `ui/server.py`'s routes and not in `BridgeDaemon`. \"What did I last see\" has **no remote surface** today; it is CLI-only (`sigil agents perceive --recall <subject>`, cli.py:452).\n\n---\n\n## 6. `sigil/mcp/server.py` — read-only MCP tools\n\n`/home/kali/sigil/sigil/mcp/server.py`, `FastMCP(\"sigil-memory\")`. The 8 `@mcp.tool()` functions:\n1. `memory_search(query, k=8)` — semantic recall, provenance (seq + entry_hash).\n2. `episodic_range(start_seq=0, end_seq=None, kind=None, limit=25)` — contiguous spine window (cap 200).\n3. `ingest_status(verify=True)` — memory health + chain/signed-head verification.\n4. `graph_entity(name)` — structural entity + neighbours + spine anchors.\n5. `graph_query(cypher, limit=50)` — **read-only** Cypher (writes/DDL refused).\n6. `threads_open(limit=25)` — open loops (grounded, un-superseded).\n7. `commitments_due(within_days=None, limit=50)` — commitment ledger.\n8. `contradictions_pending(limit=25)` — unresolved self-contradictions.\n\n**Gating:** `assert_no_offense()` at import (doctrine §12); **read-only by construction** (no write path; `graph_query` refuses writes/DDL); provenance on every result and an explicit \"memory has nothing — do not fabricate\" on empty. **Transport is STDIO** (`mcp.run()`, server.py:208) — **not a network server, no token, no HTTP.** Gating is process-local stdio + the fail-closed tool allowlist (only these 8 functions exist).\n\n**Reachability:** **stdio only** — a phone cannot speak to it over WireGuard. Surfacing these read-only views to a phone would require re-exposing them over a network transport (none exists) — i.e., **no remote path today.** (The UI's `/api/snapshot`, `/api/record`, `/api/graph*` are the loopback HTTP analogues that *do* exist.)\n\n---\n\n## Cross-cutting: `sigil/bridge/` + `sigil/mesh/` — the intended phone path (crypto shipped, transport not)\n\n**`BridgeDaemon`** (`bridge/daemon.py:30`) — desktop-side methods a phone would call once transport ships:\n- `pending() -> List[{seq,tier,kind}]` — minimal fields, **no subject leaks over the tunnel** (daemon.py:41).\n- `submit_device_approval(payload) -> int` (daemon.py:47) — appends a **device-signed** approval only if `payload[\"pubkey\"] ∈ authorized_devices` **and** `verify_approval` passes; signed `target_seq` binds (no replay).\n- `panic_engage(by=\"phone\") -> int` (daemon.py:67) — halts the mesh (kill-switch engage). **No signature for the SAFE direction**; *release stays owner-only at the desktop.*\n- `relay(text) -> str` (daemon.py:75) — `KernelDispatch().send(text)`, same WARDEN gate as voice/UI.\n- `bind_ok(addr) -> bool` (daemon.py:18) — the bind predicate transport MUST use: loopback or **private (WireGuard)** address; refuses `0.0.0.0`/unspecified/public.\n\n**Device auth model** (`mesh/registry.py`): the owner authorizes a phone's Ed25519 device key with an owner-signed `mesh.device` `state=\"authorized\"` record (`authorize_device`; `revoke_device` to remove). `authorized_devices(store, owner_pubkey)` returns the live set. The phone signs approvals with its **own** key via `DeviceApprover`; `verify_approval(record, owner_pubkey, extra_pubkeys=authorized_devices(...))` accepts it only while authorized. Trust is pinned to owner-minted keys (RP-APPROVAL-2); the phone **never holds the owner trust-root key** and can approve offline. `PushNotifier` (`bridge/notifier.py`) pushes only `{seq,tier,kind}` on newly-queued A2/A3 items.\n\n**What a phone client would need:** its Ed25519 key authorized via `mesh.authorize_device` (owner-signed, done at the PC); a **network transport** wrapping `BridgeDaemon` bound to a `bind_ok` WireGuard address (this is the missing piece — it must be built, reusing the WS-C two-plane pattern); then it drives `pending`/`submit_device_approval`/`panic_engage`/`relay`.\n\n---\n\n## Summary table — remote path status today\n\n| Surface | Auth | Loopback / WireGuard | Remote path today? |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| UI `/api/*` read + `/api/action` | printed token + Origin/Host + server-side owner signing | **loopback only** (127.0.0.1) | Only via a tunnel forwarding to loopback |\n| `KernelDispatch.send` | delegates to Rust KERNEL WARDEN gate | subprocess (no net) | Only wrapped (UI `/api/ask`, bridge `relay`) |\n| Operator/Delegate `preview`/`execute` | owner- or device-signed approval to run | local library calls | **Approve** queued item = yes (via bridge, unshipped); **execute** = PC-only |\n| Gesture `SessionGate.arm/disarm` | **local owner key in-process** | local daemon only | **NO remote arm/disarm path** |\n| Perception (Frame ingest / recall) | local devices; egress owner/device-gated | local only | **NO inbound-frame + NO recall endpoint** |\n| MCP 8 read-only tools | fail-closed allowlist, read-only, no-offense | **stdio only** | **NO network transport** |\n| `BridgeDaemon` (phone API) | owner-authorized device Ed25519 | `bind_ok` (loopback/private) | Crypto/methods shipped;
